Motivation: 从有限且含噪的焦栈重建光场是高度病态的逆问题。尽管对给定光学装置而言光场到焦栈的成像几何固定，但光场的空间-角度结构（视图内空间细节、跨视图角度依赖、视差）随场景变化，固定的预训练先验无法最优刻画每个测试光场的空间-角度结构。

Method: 提出结构感知的测试时自适应方法STAR：对每个测试光场，冻结预训练扩散先验，仅拟合三个轻量适配器到观测焦栈，以联合自适应光场空间-角度结构的三个分量（视图内空间细节、跨视图角度依赖、视差）。

Result: STAR在双焦面和三焦面两种设置下均优于现有最先进方法，并且推理时间短于采用测试时参数更新的方法。

Conclusion: STAR验证了针对每个测试样本自适应其空间-角度结构能显著提升光场重建质量，同时保持较低推理开销，为病态光场重建提供了有效的测试时自适应方案。

Abstract: Light field (LF) reconstruction from limited and noisy focal stack (FS) measurements is a highly ill-posed inverse problem. Although the LF-to-FS imaging geometry is fixed for a given optical setup, LF spatial-angular structure---including within-view spatial details, cross-view angular dependencies, and disparity across views---varies across scenes. Consequently, a fixed pre-trained prior may not optimally capture the spatial-angular structure of each test LF. We propose Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction (STAR), the first test-time adaptation framework for reconstructing an LF from FS. For each test LF, STAR freezes a pre-trained diffusion prior and fits three lightweight adapters to the observed FS to jointly adapt the three components of the LF's spatial-angular structure. STAR outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both two- and three-focal-sheet settings, with shorter inference times than those with test-time parameter updates.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmarking MLLMs via Cognitive Expected Scene Graph for Safety-Critical Visual Negation Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19767)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"安全认知下的场景否定理解"（SNUS）任务，构建负样本描述数据集并设计极性感知的结构化评估指标 CESG，以解决现有多模态大模型和指标在视觉否定语义下的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正的机器智能需要超越被动的像素配准，掌握对缺失信息进行自上而下功能推理的视觉否定理解能力。但当前无约束的视觉否定范式过于开放，且普遍存在的"肯定偏见"导致现有多模态大模型和评估指标在否定语义下完全失效。

Method: 以安全这一高度实用且关键的认知维度为锚，定义了"安全认知下的场景否定理解"（SNUS）任务；构建了一个高保真负样本描述数据集，映射局部危险的密集断言；同时提出"认知期望场景图"（CESG）评分，一种基于结构、感知极性的评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明，当前模型在 SNUS 任务上表现挣扎，而传统度量指标在语义反转下完全崩溃；相比之下，所提框架为 SNUS 提供了稳定的基准。

Conclusion: 该框架为推进风险感知的情境理解和反事实认知奠定了严谨基础。

Abstract: True machine intelligence requires transcending passive pixel registration to master top-down functional reasoning over absent information via visual negation understanding. However, unconstrained visual negation paradigms remain overly open-ended, and pervasive affirmation bias causes both existing Multi-Modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and evaluation metrics to fail under negative semantics. To solve these intertwined challenges systematically, we first anchor the boundaries of negation reasoning within specific cognitive goals. Specifically, by focusing on safety as a highly pragmatic and critical cognitive dimension, we define the task of \textbf{S}cene \textbf{N}egation \textbf{U}nderstanding under \textbf{S}afety Cognition (\textbf{SNUS}). Under this framework, we construct a high-fidelity negative caption dataset mapping dense assertions of localized hazards. Concurrently, we propose the Cognitive Expected Scene Graph (CESG) Score, a structure-grounded, polarity-aware evaluation metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while current models struggle on the task, traditional metrics completely collapse under semantic reversals. Conversely, our framework delivers a solid benchmark for SNUS, providing a rigorous foundation to advance risk-aware situational comprehension and counterfactual cognition.

</details>


### [35] [AI Smart Glasses for Wearable Intelligence: From Egocentric Sensing to Agentic Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19793)
*Xu Yuan,Yi Wang,Zhuohang Jiang,Haohao Qu,Yujuan Ding,Shanru Lin,Guoliang Xing,Hongxia Yang,Jiannong Cao,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了"AI智能眼镜"这一新兴概念，将其从单纯的拍摄显示设备重新定义为可穿戴智能平台，并围绕硬件基础、可穿戴智能、交互设计和应用场景四个维度系统梳理技术与研究现状，同时提出五大前沿挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能的进步正推动智能眼镜从自我中心（egocentric）的采集与显示设备，转型为受严格形态限制的可穿戴智能平台。然而，现有研究分散，缺乏一个统一的系统级视角来统筹第一人称感知、资源受限计算、智能推理、多模态交互与真实世界应用约束之间的协同设计。

Method: 本文以"AI智能眼镜"为系统级概念框架，采用四维度组织法进行综述：(1) 硬件基础——界定感知、计算、反馈与持续部署的能力边界；(2) 可穿戴智能——将自我中心信号转化为感知、情境与智能体（agentic）能力；(3) 交互设计——用户如何在活动进行中请求、接收、纠正与调节辅助；(4) 应用场景——覆盖医疗健康、无障碍、情境学习、日常生活辅助、文旅与工业支持等，分析领域需求如何重塑系统设计与评估。在此基础上归纳出五大跨领域挑战。

Result: 本文提出了AI智能眼镜的统一概念框架，系统梳理了四大维度的关键技术、应用与评估方法，并识别出五大交叉性研究挑战：下一代硬件、可信的自我中心智能、终身个性化记忆、主动式智能，以及具身基础模型。

Conclusion: 将智能眼镜定位为可穿戴智能平台，可以为这一新兴领域的技术、应用与开放问题提供一个统一的组织框架；未来的突破有赖于硬件、可信智能、个性化记忆、主动智能与具身基础模型等方向的协同发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) are reshaping smart glasses from egocentric capture and display devices into platforms for wearable intelligence. Smart glasses increasingly serve as wearable AI systems that connect first-person observation with real-time assistance under strict form-factor constraints. We frame this transition through the lens of \emph{AI smart glasses} and define them as a system-level concept in which egocentric sensing, resource-aware computing, intelligent reasoning, multimodal interaction, and real-world application constraints are co-designed for personalized assistance in the physical world. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around four connected dimensions. First, we examine the hardware foundation that bounds sensing, computation, feedback delivery, and sustained deployment. Second, we study wearable intelligence, where egocentric signals are transformed into perceptual, contextual, and agentic capabilities. Third, we discuss interaction design, through which users request, receive, correct, and regulate assistance during ongoing activity. Fourth, we analyze application scenarios across healthcare, accessibility, situated learning, daily life assistance, cultural tourism, and industrial support, showing how domain requirements reshape system design and evaluation. We further identify five cross-cutting research challenges for future AI smart glasses: next-generation hardware, trustworthy egocentric intelligence, lifelong personalized memory, proactive intelligence, and embodied foundation models. By centering smart glasses as wearable-intelligence platforms, this survey provides a unified framework for organizing technologies, applications, and open challenges in this emerging area.

</details>


### [36] [Absence is Presence: Understanding Visual Scene Negative Events Under Safety Cognitive Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19812)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRCD框架，以安全为认知约束，通过反事实重建与对比解码实现视觉场景负面描述（negative captioning），把物理缺失转化为语义缺失事件，为该开创性任务建立了高性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统场景理解只关注图像中客观存在的肯定性信息，但在安全关键领域，理解“应当存在却实际缺失”的信息对风险防范至关重要。现有视觉语言模型受肯定偏置（affirmation bias）抑制、心理填充能力有限、以及表示偏置（representation bias）的影响，难以将物理缺失推理为语义负面事件。

Method: 提出基于反事实重建与对比解码（CRCD）的负面描述框架：1）将任务重构为“反事实潜在变化描述”（counterfactual latent change captioning）以绕过肯定偏置，对比合成出的安全期望场景与现实以识别语义缺失；2）设计双分支反事实重建架构，amodal completion分支修复残缺物体，functional association分支推断完全缺失的安全物体；3）集成多条件表示学习机制，将通用特征投影到预定义安全准则子空间，缓解表示偏置；4）对重建场景原型与原始输入做特征级语义残差解码，约束“不存在”搜索空间并激活解码器的负面逻辑。

Result: 大量实验验证了CRCD的有效性，在该开创性任务上确立了高性能基线。

Conclusion: CRCD通过反事实重建与对比解码有效克服了肯定偏置、心理填充不足和表示偏置三大挑战，成功将物理缺失转化为语义负面事件，为安全约束下的视觉负面描述任务提供了可靠且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional scene understanding focuses on affirmative information objectively present in images. However, in safety-critical domains, comprehending key information that should exist but is actually absent is vital for risk mitigation. To bridge this gap, we focus on visual scene negative captioning with safety as the cognitive constraint. The core challenge is to convert physical absence into semantic negative events. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with this process because affirmation bias suppresses negative reasoning, while limited mental filling capability and representation bias further hinder the inference of absent information. To address these challenges, we propose a negative captioning framework based on counterfactual reconstruction and contrastive decoding (CRCD). Inspired by human cognition, CRCD reformulates the task as counterfactual latent change captioning to bypass affirmation bias. It contrasts a synthesized safe expectation with reality to identify semantic omissions. To address limited mental filling, we design a dual-branch counterfactual reconstruction architecture. The amodal completion branch restores defective objects, while the functional association branch infers completely absent safety objects. Concurrently, a multi-condition representation learning mechanism is integrated to mitigate representation bias by projecting universal features onto predefined safety criteria subspaces, thereby capturing information across more dimensions. By decoding feature-level semantic residuals between the reconstructed scene prototype and raw input, CRCD bounds the non-existence search space and activates the decoder's negative logic. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of CRCD, establishing a high-performance baseline for this pioneering task.

</details>


### [37] [SnapPhysics: A Physics-Aware Scene Graph from a Single View for Interactive Mixed Reality Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19815)
*Suji Kang,Seok-Young Kim,Young Bin Kim,Taewook Ha,Dieter Schmalstieg,Shohei Mori,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SnapPhysics 是一个免训练框架，能从单张图像同时重建 3D 物体并估计其质量、摩擦系数、重心等物理属性。它结合实例级 3D 重建、空间对齐和物理感知场景图，为 VLM 推理提供几何与关系上下文，解决现有方法（视频动力学分析计算开销大、VLM 缺乏几何依据）的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合现实（MR）中实现物理一致的交互，质量、摩擦、重心等物理属性与几何形状同样重要。现有方法要么通过分析视频中的物体动力学来推断属性，计算成本高；要么对单张图像直接查询视觉语言模型（VLM），但缺乏几何依据和物体间关系，导致估计不准。

Method: SnapPhysics 将实例级 3D 重建与空间对齐相结合，构建物理感知场景图来编码物体间关系以及每个物体的度量几何信息，作为结构化上下文提供给 VLM 进行属性推理，整个过程免训练。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上，SnapPhysics 的场景级 F-Score 比最佳基于学习的方法提升 18.6%；在带有真实质量标注的实拍场景上，相比仅用 VLM 的估计，平均绝对对数差误差（mALDE）最多降低 20.5%，对数尺度相关性（r²_ls）最多提升 19.6%。

Conclusion: SnapPhysics 无需人工调参即可实现物理可交互的 MR 体验，证明了通过结合 3D 重建、空间对齐和物理感知场景图来增强 VLM 推理的有效性。

Abstract: We propose SnapPhysics, a training-free framework that reconstructs 3D objects and estimates their physical properties such as mass, friction, and center of gravity from a single image. For physically coherent interactions in mixed reality (MR), such properties are as important as geometry. Prior approaches infer them by analyzing object dynamics in video, which is computationally costly, or by querying vision-language models (VLMs) on single images, which lacks geometric grounding and inter-object relationships. We address these limitations by combining instance-level 3D reconstruction and spatial alignment with a physics-aware scene graph that encodes these relationships and per-object metric geometry as structured context for VLM-based property reasoning. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that SnapPhysics improves scene-level F-Score by 18.6% over the best learning-based method, and on real captured scenes with ground-truth mass, it reduces the mean absolute log difference error (mALDE) by up to 20.5% and improves log-scale correlation ($r^2_{\mathrm{ls}}$) by up to 19.6% over VLM-only estimation. SnapPhysics enables physically interactive MR experiences without manual parameter tuning. Project page: https://snapphysics-ismar2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [38] [KoUniTalk: A Lightweight Articulation-Centered Korean-English 3D Talking Face Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19840)
*Hyunjung Chung,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KoUniTalk 是一个轻量级、以发音为中心的韩英 3D 说话人脸基准，通过变形迁移将 VOCASET 与韩国语音驱动 3D 数据统一到共享网格拓扑，大幅降低输出维度，并支持跨语言发音评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量 3D 说话人脸数据集以英语为主，而韩国 3D 面部运动数据因网格拓扑、空间尺度、坐标系和时序采样上的差异，难以与标准英语基准直接结合，阻碍了跨语言的语音驱动面部动画训练与评测。

Method: 利用变形迁移（deformation transfer）将 VOCASET 和公开的韩国语音驱动 3D 说话人脸数据重定向到共享网格拓扑，构建身份中性的规范输出空间。统一模板含 1,176 个顶点，聚焦嘴部及相邻的中下面部区域，将输出维度分别从 15,069(VOCASET/FLAME) 和 72,147(原始韩国网格) 降至 3,528 维，实现 4.27 倍和 20.45 倍降维。同时评估语义口部关键点轨迹（张嘴度、嘴宽、张宽比、张嘴动态），并因韩国数据集官方测试集未公开而额外定义了主体不相交的基准划分。

Result: 处理后的匹配基准包含 22 位说话人、4,978 个序列、642,781 帧，可在单一紧凑的发音模板空间中实现韩英跨数据集的语音驱动 3D 面部动画模型评测。源数据所报告的库存数量与这些处理后数量分别列出。

Conclusion: KoUniTalk 不提出新的变形迁移算法或全头保身份虚拟人数据集，而是为跨英语与韩语的受控语音驱动面部发音训练和评估提供一个身份中性的统一输出空间，降低维度并保留语音相关运动，促进跨语言 3D 面部动画研究。

Abstract: High-quality 3D talking face datasets remain largely English- centric, and Korean 3D facial motion data are difficult to combine with standard English benchmarks because of differences in mesh topology, spatial scale, coordinate system, and temporal sampling. We present KoUniTalk, a lightweight articulation-centered Korean-English 3D talk- ing face benchmark that retargets VOCASET and the released Korean speech-based 3D talking face data to a shared mesh topology using de- formation transfer. Rather than proposing a new deformation-transfer algorithm or a full-head identity-preserving avatar dataset, KoUniTalk provides an identity-neutral canonical output space for controlled speech- driven facial articulation training and evaluation across English and Ko- rean. The unified template contains 1,176 vertices and focuses on the mouth and adjacent lower- and mid-face regions, reducing the output dimensionality from 15,069 and 72,147 dimensions to 3,528 dimensions, corresponding to 4.27-fold and 20.45-fold reductions compared with VO- CASET/FLAME and the original Korean mesh, respectively. To exam- ine whether retargeting preserves speech-relevant motion, we evaluate semantic mouth-landmark trajectories, including mouth opening, mouth width, aperture ratio, and mouth-opening dynamics. Since the official test set of the Korean dataset is not publicly released, we additionally define a subject-disjoint Korean benchmark split. The processed matched benchmark contains 22 speakers, 4,978 sequences, and 642,781 frames, enabling Korean-English cross-dataset evaluation of speech-driven 3D fa- cial animation models in a single compact articulation-template space. Source-reported inventory counts are listed separately from these pro- cessed counts

</details>


### [39] [PACE: Precise AI Cinematic Expression: A Typed Specification for Script-Grounded Previsualization and Geometric Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19853)
*Bing Duan,Qiang Guo,Linpu Li,Zhijian Mao,Min Zhu,Zhirui Ren,Yiwei Yan,Xi Chu,Xiaoding Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE 提出一种类型化的电影表达规划表示，将剧本证据、角色/道具/位置、站位与运镜写成结构化值并按层级继承，编译同时生成扩散模型提示词和以米为单位的 3D 场景，由相机求解器落实取景，并用几何度量逐字段核验声明与成品之间的偏差，而非依赖模型主观判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从剧本到电影之间存在一个先于一切的空间规划问题（谁站在哪里、相机从哪里看到什么）；直接用自然语言要求图像扩散模型生成镜头，会让模型以自身默认值擅自决定这一规划，导致构图不可控、不可验证。

Method: 设计 PACE 类型系统，将值按脚本、场景、镜头、画格四级层级写入并向下继承；编写编译器把该表示同时编译为扩散模型提示词与以米为单位的 3D 场景；相机求解器摆放相机使声明的取景即所建取景；凡声明值落为几何后，逐字段测量编译后相机与舞台渲染相对声明值的偏离，而非让模型打分。

Result: 在 11 场景的 Automatic Drive 剧本上，每个单主体舞台化画格的主体位置误差在帧宽 1.2% 以内；两或三主体时单一相机位姿无法同时满足所有位置，残差被显式报告而非被吞掉。在 204 个外部导演-分镜镜头上，成片头部高度是导演文字舞台目标的 1.906 倍、编译提示词的 1.733 倍、灰色盒对照的 0.955 倍——最保取景的条件反而不如画动作。对 30 个镜头显式声明位姿，动作描绘率从 58.9% 提升至 74.4% 且不移动取景。

Conclusion: PACE 使空间规划可声明、可编译、可度量，证明显式声明位姿能在保持取景的同时显著提升动作描绘准确度；过渡转场、运动拟合与生成画格的人工审校仍有待解决。

Abstract: Between a screenplay and a film sits a planning problem that is spatial first: who stands where, and what a camera sees from where it stands. An image diffusion model asked for a shot in free text settles that plan by its own defaults. We present PACE (Precise AI Cinematic Expression), a typed representation for the plan: the screenplay evidence, the characters, props and locations it needs, where each subject stands, and what the camera does. A value is written once at the level it belongs to (script, scene, shot or panel) and inherited below it. A compiler turns the result into both the prompt sent to the diffusion model and a 3D scene built in metres, and a camera solver places the camera so that the declared framing is the framing built. Where a declared value becomes geometry, PACE measures, field by field, how far the compiled camera and the staged render sit from the declaration, rather than asking a model to judge.
  On the 11-scene Automatic Drive screenplay, every staged single-subject panel places its subject within 1.2% of frame width of its declared position; with two or three subjects one camera pose cannot satisfy every position, and the residual is reported rather than absorbed. On 204 external director-storyboard shots, delivered head height is 1.906 times the staged target from the director's words, 1.733 from the compiled prompt, and 0.955 with the greybox control; the condition that holds framing best draws the described action least. Declaring the pose on 30 shots raises the action drawn from 58.9% to 74.4% without moving the framing. Transitions, fitted motion and human review of the generated panels remain open. Code: https://github.com/StudioPiLabs/pace-core

</details>


### [40] [Socialized UAV Cross-Task Learning: Towards Cross-Granularity Collaboration through Hierarchical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19867)
*Xinjie Yao,Ruipu Zhao,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Zhoupeng Guo,Weihao Li,Zhen Wang,Qilong Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨粒度多任务联合学习中的干扰与协作失衡问题，提出CrossUAV无人机基准与跨粒度社会化协作框架CGSC，通过渐进式、自适应的层级交互调节，在检测与实例分割任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨任务异构学习（如UAV感知中的目标检测与实例分割）常通过特征共享、蒸馏或辅助监督进行耦合，但表示粒度与监督粒度的不匹配易导致任务间干扰、教师偏置或单向崩塌。作者认为根本问题在于层级交互的调节，而非简单的任务耦合，因此需要系统研究跨粒度协作机制。

Method: 提出CrossUAV基准用于无人机联合检测与实例分割的统一评估；提出跨粒度社会化协作（CGSC）框架，在网络层级上渐进式激活跨任务交互，并根据任务贡献自适应调节交互强度，从而抑制有害干扰、利用粗细粒度的互补结构。

Result: 大量实验表明CGSC在检测和实例分割两个任务上均取得一致提升，验证了层级动态交互作为跨粒度协作机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨粒度学习应被建模为层级交互调节问题；渐进且自适应的交互控制能有效缓解干扰并促进粗细粒度知识互补，为跨粒度多任务协作提供了有效机制。

Abstract: Joint learning across heterogeneous tasks is often treated as task coupling through feature sharing, distillation, or auxiliary supervision. However, in cross-task learning, mismatched representational and supervisory granularities make such coupling prone to interference, teacher bias, or unidirectional collapse. We argue that cross-granularity learning is fundamentally a problem of hierarchical interaction regulation rather than simple task coupling. This issue is particularly evident in UAV perception, where visual shifts and detection--segmentation objectives naturally form coarse- and fine-grained knowledge sources. To systematically study this problem, we introduce CrossUAV, a UAV benchmark for joint object detection and instance segmentation that provides a unified evaluation platform for cross-granularity task collaboration. To address these challenges, we propose Cross-Granularity Socialized Collaboration (CGSC), a progressive and adaptive framework that regulates when, where, and how tasks exchange information across network hierarchies. CGSC progressively activates cross-task interactions and adaptively adjusts the strength according to task contribution, suppressing harmful interference while exploiting complementary coarse- and fine-grained structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on both tasks, validating hierarchical dynamic interaction as an effective mechanism for cross-granularity collaboration.

</details>


### [41] [BINDER: A Latent Variable Model for Probabilistic Medical Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19875)
*Stefano Cerri,Amirhossein Hassankhani,Yaël Balbastre,Koen Van Leemput*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息准则的通用医学图像配准概率模型，核心是假设潜在体素对应的空间插值技术，据此推导出仅含闭式迭代更新的优化与MCMC采样方法，实现类demons的高效非线性配准算法及多模态高维3D形变的不确定性量化（BINDER）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像配准方法在通用性（尤其是多模态）与不确定性量化方面存在不足：基于互信息的配准缺乏高效的概率推断框架，且难以在多模态、高维3D形变场景下量化配准不确定性。作者希望提出一种既保留互信息通用配准能力、又能进行高效优化和贝叶斯采样的统一概率模型。

Method: 提出以互信息配准准则为基础的概率模型，核心是一种假设待配准图像间存在潜在体素级对应（latent voxel-wise correspondences）的空间插值技术。利用这些潜在变量推导出专用优化与MCMC采样技术，其更新均为闭式迭代形式。在非线性配准中，得到类demons的高效优化算法，并构建对应采样器用于贝叶斯推断。

Result: 所得到的优化算法在多种单模态与多模态配准任务上展现出鲁棒的即用（out-of-the-box）性能；对应的采样器首次实现在极高维3D形变的多模态配准场景中对不确定性进行量化。代码BINDER已开源。

Conclusion: 通过引入潜在体素对应并将互信息纳入概率框架，实现了统一且可扩展的配准方案，既提供高效鲁棒的点估计非线性配准，又支持多模态高维3D形变的不确定性量化，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a new probabilistic model for general-purpose medical image registration that builds upon the mutual information registration criterion. It centers around a spatial interpolation technique that assumes latent voxel-wise correspondences between the images being registered. By exploiting these latent variables, we derive dedicated optimization and MCMC sampling techniques that only involve closed-form iterative updates. When applied to nonlinear registration, an efficient demons-like optimization algorithm is obtained that shows robust out-of-the-box performance across a variety of monomodal and multimodal registration tasks. We also demonstrate a corresponding sampler that can quantify, for the first time, uncertainty in multimodal registration scenarios with very high-dimensional 3D deformations. Our code, which we call BINDER (Bayesian INference for DEformable Registration), is freely available at https://github.com/ste93ste/BINDER.

</details>


### [42] [SlugTrails: An Egocentric Benchmark for Floor Plan Localization in Large Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19876)
*Yunqian Cheng,Roberto Manduchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlugTrails 是一个面向大型室内空间的基于平面图（floor plan）的室内视觉定位基准，包含三个校园建筑、六层楼（22089 m² 平面轮廓）的 30 Hz Aria 眼镜第一人称数据、由 CAD 衍生的带语义类别与通行空间掩码的平面图，以及经激光测量锚点对齐到平面图坐标系的轨迹。该基准提供三种受限视场下采集几何信息的协议（单帧行走、静止多视角扫描、带里程计的行走流），用于统一比较不同设计定位方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于平面图的室内视觉定位方法大多在小型住宅环境中开发和评测，而真实部署场景是大型公共建筑，二者存在显著差距，因此需要一个更大规模、更贴近真实第一人称感知的基准来揭示并推动该领域发展。

Method: 作者构建 SlugTrails 基准：使用 Aria 眼镜在三座校园建筑六层楼上以 30 Hz 记录第一人称视频，生成由 CAD 派生的带语义类别与通行空间掩码的平面图，并用激光测量锚点将轨迹对齐到平面图坐标系。基准定义三种统一协议（单个行走帧、静止多视角扫描、带里程计的行走流），并在五种代表性几何方法和学习方法上，分别以其原生传感配置进行评估，包括加载官方发布权重的"stock checkpoint"及在 SlugTrails 上微调/从头训练的对比实验，另在 LaMAR 上做跨数据集泛化验证。

Result: 官方发布的预训练权重在 SlugTrails 上几乎失效（行走单帧上最高仅 0.004 R@1m30°）；而在 SlugTrails 上微调可在全部三个任务上提升所有可训练方法（如 F³Loc 单帧由 0.0 提升到 0.141，序列由 0.03 提升到 0.66），且收益随观测累积而增大。相同的微调权重还在未使用 LaMAR 训练的情况下改善了在 LaMAR 上的跨数据集泛化（F³Loc 序列 R@1m 由 0.048 提升到 0.143，UnLoc 由 0.063 提升到 0.127）；相比之下，仅在 SlugTrails 上从头训练的效果远低于在官方权重上微调，表明当前瓶颈在于室内数据而非模型架构。

Conclusion: SlugTrails 表明基于平面图的室内视觉定位在大型真实空间上仍极具挑战，现有预训练模型难以直接迁移；在更大规模室内数据上微调能显著提升性能并增强跨数据集泛化，说明提升此类定位的关键是扩充室内数据而非重新设计架构。作者已公开数据集、协议与工具。

Abstract: Floor-plan-based indoor visual localization enables infrastructure-free positioning, but most methods are developed and evaluated in small residential environments unlike the large public buildings of real deployment. We introduce SlugTrails, a floor plan localization benchmark for large indoor spaces under realistic egocentric sensing: $30$ Hz Aria glasses recordings across three campus buildings and six floors ($22089$ m$^2$ of floor plan outline), CAD-derived floor plans with semantic classes and circulation space masks, and trajectories aligned into the floor plan frame using laser-surveyed anchors. One protocol covers three practical ways of gathering geometry under a limited field of view -- a single walking frame, a stationary multi-view sweep, and a walking stream with odometry -- so methods designed for different regimes are compared on the same buildings and ground truth. Evaluating five representative geometric and learned systems under their native sensing configurations, we find that stock checkpoints (official released weights) are near zero on SlugTrails (at most $0.004$ R@1m30$^{\circ}$ on walking single frames), while fine-tuning on SlugTrails improves every trainable family on all three tasks (e.g., F$^3$Loc $0.0 \rightarrow 0.141$ single-frame and $0.03 \rightarrow 0.66$ sequential), with gains compounding as observations accumulate. The same fine-tuned weights also improve cross-dataset generalization on LaMAR with no LaMAR training (sequential R@1m $0.048 \rightarrow 0.143$ for F$^3$Loc and $0.063 \rightarrow 0.127$ for UnLoc), whereas train-from-scratch on SlugTrails alone stays far below fine-tuning from stock weights -- evidence that floor plan localization is currently limited by indoor data rather than by architecture. We release the dataset, protocols, and tools at https://github.com/Head-inthe-Cloud/SlugTrails.

</details>


### [43] [BinoGen: Scaling egocentric binocular data for embodied visual perception and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19881)
*Chunpeng Li,Ya-tang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BinoGen 是一个自动化框架，用于在室内环境中大规模生成具有体现(embodiment)意识的自我中心双目视觉经验，并附带密集多模态标注；基于其构建的 2000 万+图像数据集可提升真实世界感知任务，并支持研究观察者体现对感知学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉感知依赖连续的视觉经验，但大规模采集自我中心双目观察及密集标注成本高、难度大；且视觉经验既受环境影响，也受观察者体现（视高、视场、双目几何、运动方式）影响。现有方法难以同时建模这两种变异，缺乏可控、可扩展的数据生成方案。

Method: 提出 BinoGen 自动化生成框架，通过生成式场景合成、概率化物体实例化、外观随机化、随机轨迹生成和可配置双目相机设置，联合建模环境与观察者变异；生成同步双目视频及密集多模态监督（深度图、光流、表面法线、语义图、物体坐标、相机位姿）。利用该框架构建了超过 2000 万张标注图像的数据集。

Result: 将 BinoGen 数据加入训练可持续提升真实世界视觉感知性能，包括深度估计、物体检测和视频目标跟踪。此外，同一环境下配对的"人类启发"与"小鼠启发"观察数据，使得可受控研究观察者体现对感知学习的影响：体现特异性适配显著提升性能，而联合训练使单一模型能在两种体现下均具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 大规模、可控的视觉经验生成能够有效改善具身感知。BinoGen 不仅能提升真实世界感知任务的性能，还为研究观察者体现对感知学习的影响提供了受控实验平台。

Abstract: Embodied visual perception relies on temporally coherent visual experience accumulated through continuous engagement with the environment. However, collecting large-scale egocentric binocular observations together with dense annotations remains costly and difficult. Moreover, visual experience is shaped not only by the environment but also by the embodiment of the observer, including viewing height, field of view, binocular geometry, and motion through the scene. To address these challenges, we present BinoGen, an automated framework for generating large-scale, embodiment-aware egocentric binocular visual experiences in indoor environments. BinoGen jointly models environmental and observer variation through generative scene synthesis, probabilistic object instantiation, appearance randomization, stochastic trajectory generation, and configurable binocular camera setups. The framework produces synchronized binocular videos together with dense multimodal supervision, including depth maps, optical flow, surface normals, semantic maps, object coordinates, and camera poses. Using BinoGen, we construct a dataset comprising more than 20 million annotated images for supervised learning. We demonstrate two complementary utilities of BinoGen. First, incorporating BinoGen data consistently improves real-world visual perception, including depth estimation, object detection, and video object tracking. Second, paired human-inspired and mouse-inspired observations from the same environments enable controlled investigation of how observer embodiment affects perceptual learning. Embodiment-specific adaptation substantially improves performance, while joint training enables a single model to perform competitively across both embodiments. Together, these results demonstrate that large-scale, controllable visual experience can improve embodied perception...

</details>


### [44] [CitySTAR: Structured and Topology-Aware Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Urban 3D Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19911)
*Shuai Zhang,Hongye Hou,Qinghe Liu,Zhuoxiao Li,Dongli Wu,Jing Ou,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CitySTAR，一个免训练、推理驱动的城市场景 3D 定位框架，将城市级 3D 定位重构为结构化约束推理，并配套发布增强基准 CitySTAR-3D，在开放世界城市 3D 定位上取得持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 定位方法主要依赖特征相似度或直接匹配，难以将自然语言意图与十亿级城市点云中隐含的语义和几何结构关联起来，因而亟需一种能显式建模跨模态约束与空间结构的推理式定位方法。

Method: CitySTAR 将城市级 3D 定位重构为结构化约束推理：通过 CodeLLM 驱动的工具将描述语义组织为可计算的跨模态约束（覆盖开放词汇 3D 实体、属性与空间关系）。具体流程为：① 将十亿级点云提升为可查询的开放词汇 3D 实例场景图，并用 CodeLLM 工具为节点属性与 3D 空间关系提供多模态证据；② 用成对超图建模目标-上下文拓扑，并进行双向拓扑验证以实现结构消歧；③ Reflective Cross-modal Grounding 模块融合拓扑一致性与以候选为中心的 2D 视觉证据，在度量感知的 3D 上下文图上做出决策。

Result: 作者引入增强基准 CitySTAR-3D，在语义覆盖、实例完整性、包围盒保真度和空间关系复杂度方面均优于以往基准；大量实验表明 CitySTAR 在开放世界城市 3D 定位上持续提升，同时保持强可解释性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: CitySTAR 表明将城市级 3D 定位建模为结构化约束推理是有效的，通过免训练的图推理与反思式跨模态证据融合，实现了可解释、可泛化的开放世界城市 3D 定位，并为后续研究提供了更具挑战性的 CitySTAR-3D 基准。

Abstract: 3D grounding aims to localize target entities in complex scenes from natural language and plays a fundamental role in embodied perception and spatial reasoning. However, existing approaches mostly rely on feature similarity or direct matching, making it difficult to connect natural-language intent with the implicit semantic and geometric structures hidden in billion-scale urban point clouds. We reformulate city-scale 3D grounding as structured constraint reasoning, where description semantics are organized into computable cross-modal constraints over open-vocabulary 3D entities, attributes, and spatial relations. We present CitySTAR, a training-free framework for reasoning-driven urban 3D grounding. CitySTAR lifts raw billion-scale urban point clouds into a query-ready scene graph of open-vocabulary 3D instances, with CodeLLM-driven tools supplying multimodal evidence for node attributes and 3D spatial relations. It then models target-context topology with paired hypergraphs and performs bidirectional topology verification for structural disambiguation. Finally, a Reflective Cross-modal Grounding module integrates topology consistency and candidate-centered 2D visual evidence to make decisions over a metric-aware 3D context graph. To further support this setting, we introduce CitySTAR-3D, an enhanced benchmark that improves semantic coverage, instance completeness, bounding-box fidelity, and spatial-relation complexity in city-scale 3D grounding. Extensive experiments show that CitySTAR consistently improves open-world urban 3D grounding while maintaining strong interpretability and generalization.

</details>


### [45] [DirtyMoCap: Robust Motion Capture from Unconstrained Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19927)
*Long Wang,Shuting Zhao,Shen Yan,Siyuan Yu,Xiaoben Li,Zeyu Cai,Yumeng Hou,Yuliang Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirtyMoCap 提出一种无标记布局依赖的鲁棒框架，将无序、稀疏、嘈杂的光学动作捕捉标记点映射到固定的"代理锚点"（骨骼关节点+体表点），再通过可微高斯-牛顿求解器拟合 SMPL-H 模型，恢复全身姿态、位移与体型，并在任意标记布局下超越专用基线，速率提升可达百倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学动捕依赖严格的标记点布局与干净轨迹，难以应对现实中稀疏、嘈杂、无序且配置未知/多变的原始标记数据，需要一种能跨越任意布局、从受损原始标记直接重建参数化人体模型的通用方案。

Method: 核心思路是将无序标记观测映射到固定的"代理锚点"（骨骼关节点与体表点）作为稳定中间表示：先采用循环滑动窗口架构对长序列进行锚点初始化与跟踪；再利用自定义可微高斯-牛顿求解器将 SMPL-H 模型拟合到跟踪锚点，恢复姿态、位移与体型。通过显式推导几何残差，求解器端到端学习自适应观测置信度、平滑性与先验权重，动态适配输入数据可靠性。

Result: 在多种嘈杂标记配置上的大量实验表明，DirtyMoCap 仅用单一训练模型即可跨任意布局泛化，在关节与顶点重建精度上均持续优于最先进的配置专用基线；自定义 CUDA 求解器相较标准 PyTorch 实现最高加速 100 倍。此外，该方法应用于传统中国武术的异构原始光学动捕记录，生成了时间连贯的 SMPL-H 重建武术动作数据集。

Conclusion: DirtyMoCap 成功弥合了受损原始标记与参数化人体模型之间的鸿沟，实现了无标记布局、对噪声与稀疏鲁棒且高效的通用人体动捕重建，并公开代码与数据以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Optical motion capture delivers high-fidelity human motion, but its reliance on strict marker layouts and clean trajectories severely limits its real-world applicability. In practice, tracking systems frequently output unconstrained markers: sparse, noisy, and unordered point clouds with unknown or varying configurations. To bridge the gap between corrupted raw markers and parametric human models, we introduce DirtyMoCap, a robust, marker-layout-free framework. Our core insight is to map unordered marker observations to a fixed set of "proxy anchors" comprising skeletal joints and body surface points, which serve as a stable intermediate representation. We first initialize and track these anchors over long sequences using a recurrent sliding-window architecture. Then, a custom differentiable Gauss-Newton solver fits the SMPL-H model to the tracked anchors to recover full-body pose, translation, and shape. By explicitly deriving geometric residuals, our solver learns adaptive observation confidence, smoothness, and prior weights end-to-end, adapting dynamically to the reliability of the input data. Extensive experiments on diverse, noisy marker configurations demonstrate that DirtyMoCap successfully generalizes across arbitrary layouts using only a single trained model. It consistently outperforms state-of-the-art configuration-specific baselines in both joint and vertex reconstruction accuracy, while our custom CUDA solver achieves up to a 100x speedup over standard PyTorch implementations. We further apply DirtyMoCap to heterogeneous raw optical MoCap recordings of traditional Chinese martial arts, yielding a Kung Fu motion dataset of temporally coherent SMPL-H reconstructions. Code and data are available at https://wanglongzju.github.io/DirtyMoCap-Project-Page.

</details>


### [46] [Enhanced Knowledge Distillation for Detection Transformer via Teacher Prediction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19964)
*Yitong Xing,Yuhao Cheng,Yanping Li,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对DETR蒸馏中教师监督质量不足的问题，提出即插即用的教师预测精化蒸馏模块TPRD，通过正样本修正（PPC）、负样本抑制（NPS）和最大暗知识保留（MDKP）提升教师监督质量，在COCO和VOC上验证了有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DETR因计算成本高难以部署到边缘设备；现有DETR蒸馏方法主要关注对齐蒸馏点，却忽视了教师监督本身的质量。作者观察到DETR存在分阶段非单调预测行为：早期阶段定位良好或分类正确的预测可能在后期退化，某些负预测则变得越来越过度自信。仅依赖当前阶段的预测会得到不准确且不一致的监督。

Method: 提出即插即用的教师预测精化蒸馏模块TPRD，利用分阶段预测信息在蒸馏前精化教师预测。具体包括三个组件：(1) 正样本修正PPC，通过恢复早期阶段更准确的正预测来修正退化的正预测，保证可靠的定位与分类信号；(2) 负样本抑制NPS，抑制过度自信负样本的影响，防止其提供误导性监督；(3) 最大暗知识保留MDKP，选择性精化目标类logit，同时保留非目标类之间的关系以保存信息性暗知识。

Result: 在MS COCO和PASCAL VOC上的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。代码开源：https://github.com/xingyitong1/TPRD。

Conclusion: TPRD通过改进教师预测质量而非仅对齐蒸馏点，有效解决了DETR分阶段非单调预测导致的监督不准确与不一致问题，为DETR在资源受限设备上的高效蒸馏部署提供了即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Detection Transformers (DETRs) achieve strong performance in object detection but remain challenging to deploy on edge devices due to their high computational cost. Existing DETR distillation methods mainly focus on aligning distillation points, while largely overlooking the quality of the teacher's supervision itself. We observe that due to stage-wise non-monotonic prediction behavior in DETRs, well-localized or correctly classified predictions from earlier stages may degrade in later ones, and some negative predictions become increasingly overconfident. As a result, relying solely on the current stage's predictions yields inaccurate and inconsistent supervision. To address this issue, we propose Teacher Prediction Refinement Distillation (TPRD), a plug-and-play module that refines teacher predictions before distillation by exploiting stage-wise prediction information. TPRD improves supervision quality through Positive Prediction Correction (PPC), which corrects degraded positive predictions by restoring more accurate ones from earlier stages, ensuring reliable localization and classification signals, and Negative Prediction Suppression (NPS) suppresses the influence of overconfident negatives, preventing them from providing misleading supervision to the student. To preserve informative dark knowledge, we further introduce Maximum Dark Knowledge Preservation (MDKP), which selectively refines target-class logits while retaining non-target relations. Extensive experiments on MS COCO and PASCAL VOC demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. Our code is available at https://github.com/xingyitong1/TPRD.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond the Foreground: FOV-Aware Polyp Image Synthesis via Lesion-Guided Adaptive Mucosal Context Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19966)
*Tong Wang,Yuting He,Bin Ren,Yutong Xie,Guanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LAMP，首个基于病变引导的自适应黏膜上下文传播的息肉图像合成前景引导框架，通过 FOV 掩码分离病变/有效黏膜/镜头外部，并利用交叉注意力与 FOV 约束的多向上下文传播提升合成质量，在五个息肉数据集上显著优于现有方法并提升下游分割模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜标注稀缺，合成图像-掩码对可缓解此问题，但现有前景引导方法将所有非前景像素视为背景并主要依赖局部整合，直接应用会导致两个问题：非黏膜黑色区域污染生成组织，局部推理造成黏膜纹理与光照不一致。

Method: 提出 LAMP 框架：① 使用 FOV 掩码显式分离病变、有效黏膜和镜头外部；② Lesion-to-Mucosa 交叉注意力为有效黏膜位置提取病变外观条件；③ FOV 约束的多向 Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value（VRWKV）在合法组织支撑上传播这些条件；④ 自适应门控控制其残差融合进扩散 U-Net。

Result: 在五个息肉数据集上的大量实验表明，LAMP 在整体生成质量上大幅优于现有方法，并一致提升了五个下游分割模型的性能。

Conclusion: LAMP 作为首个前景引导的息肉图像合成框架，通过显式分离 FOV 区域与自适应黏膜上下文传播，解决了黑色区域污染与纹理/光照不一致问题，验证了其对生成质量与下游分割任务的有效性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Synthetic image and mask pairs can alleviate scarce colonoscopy annotations, but realistic synthesis requires preserving the supplied lesion while generating compatible mucosa. Existing foreground-guided methods treat all non-foreground pixels as background and rely mainly on local integration. Directly applying them to colonoscopy causes two problems: non-mucosal black regions contaminate generated tissue, and local reasoning produces inconsistent mucosal texture and illumination. We propose LAMP, the first foreground-guided framework for polyp image synthesis based on lesion-guided adaptive mucosal context propagation. LAMP explicitly separates the lesion, valid mucosa, and camera exterior using a field-of-view (FOV) mask. Lesion-to-Mucosa cross-attention extracts lesion appearance conditions for valid-mucosa locations, while FOV-constrained multidirectional Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value propagates them over legal tissue support. An adaptive gate then controls their residual fusion into the diffusion U-Net. Extensive experiments on five polyp datasets demonstrate that LAMP substantially outperforms existing methods in overall generation quality and consistently improves five downstream segmentation models. Our code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/LAMP.

</details>


### [48] [An Event Preserving Velocity Invariant Representation for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19973)
*Mikihiro Ikura,Luna Gava,Jiahang Wu,Chiara Bartolozzi,Arren Glover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCARF（Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields），一种实时、速度不变的表示方法，在保留原始事件的同时高效处理快速运动、静止场景和独立运动物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机凭借异步、独立的像素电路实现了低延迟和高时间分辨率的感知，但同时带来了新的算法挑战。现有速度不变表示虽能缓解慢速运动下的观测缺失和快速运动下的运动模糊，但多数将事件转换为类图像表示，丢失了时间信息。因此需要一个保留原始事件且能一致处理各种运动情形的实时速度不变表示方法。

Method: 提出 Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields（SCARF），一种实时速度不变表示。该表示保留原始事件（而非转换为类图像），并能一致处理快速运动、静止场景以及独立移动物体等情形。

Result: SCARF 在计算效率和表示质量两方面均达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: SCARF 通过保留原始事件并结合速度不变性，解决了事件相机在各类运动场景下的表示难题，同时兼顾实时性、计算效率和表示质量，为机器人等实时视觉任务提供了有效的事件表示方案。

Abstract: Event cameras provide low-latency, high temporal resolution perception for real-time vision tasks such as robotics.The novel circuitry (i.e. asynchronous, independent pixels) that enables these advantages also introduces new algorithmic challenges. Velocity-invariant representations alleviate missing observations under slow motion and motion blur under fast motion, but most discard temporal information by converting events into image-like representations. We propose Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields (SCARF), a real-time velocity-invariant representation that preserves raw events while consistently handling fast motion, stationary scenes, and independently moving objects. SCARF achieves state-of-the-art performance in both computational efficiency and representation quality.

</details>


### [49] [QCPruner: Query-Conditioned Population Coverage for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19990)
*Shengli He,Yongchao Liang,Roumeng He,Junjie Zeng,Jiyuan He,Can Wu,Li Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 QCPruner，一种无需训练的视觉 token 剪枝方法，通过双边效用加权使视觉目标与候选代表均以查询为条件，并在视觉亲和覆盖下构建单调子模的设施选址目标，从而在保留 (1-1/e) 贪心保证的同时，在多个 MLLM 上以最低 token 预算取得最高平均相对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中视觉 token 负载过高，促使采用免训练剪枝以减少后层计算。但在固定预算下，剪枝必须既保留与查询相关的证据，又避免冗余。现有方法仅对 token 排序、多样化所选子集或优化覆盖，缺乏一个共享的"逐视觉查询效用"来同时加权视觉目标与候选代表。

Method: QCPruner 通过双边效用加权使视觉目标与候选代表两个角色都以查询为条件。具体地，利用关键词匹配的查询锚点，融合两种跨模态线索得到效用，并将其同时应用于视觉目标与候选代表，在基于视觉亲和力的覆盖框架内进行加权。由此得到的非负设施选址目标满足单调性与子模性，保留标准 (1-1/e) 贪心保证，且无需训练或参数更新。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-Video 和 Qwen2.5-VL 上，QCPruner 在每一个报告过的 token 预算下均取得所有评估的完整系统剪枝方法中最高的平均相对性能。在 LLaVA-1.5-7B（576 中 32 token）上保留 96.1% 的未剪枝性能（最强基线为 93.9%）；在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B（1296 中 256 token）上对应值分别为 96.7% 与 92.5%。

Conclusion: QCPruner 通过将查询条件化的双边效用加权引入免训练视觉剪枝，有效解决了固定预算下证据保留与去冗余的权衡问题，在不增加训练/参数开销的前提下于多种 MLLM 上实现了一致的性能领先。

Abstract: The high visual-token load in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) motivates training-free pruning to reduce later-layer computation, but under a fixed budget, pruning must preserve query-relevant evidence while avoiding redundancy. Existing methods rank tokens, diversify selected subsets, or optimize coverage without using a shared per-visual query utility to weight both visual targets and candidate representatives. We introduce QCPruner, which makes both roles query-conditioned through bilateral utility weighting. Using keyword-matched query anchors, QCPruner fuses two cross-modal cues into utility and applies it to both visual targets and candidate representatives within visual-affinity-based coverage. The resulting nonnegative facility-location objective is monotone and submodular, retains the standard (1-1/e) greedy guarantee, and requires no model training or parameter updates. Across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-Video, and Qwen2.5-VL, QCPruner achieves the highest average relative performance among evaluated complete-system pruning methods at every reported token budget. At 32 of 576 tokens on LLaVA-1.5-7B, it retains 96.1% of unpruned performance, versus 93.9% for the strongest evaluated baseline. At 256 of 1296 tokens on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the corresponding values are 96.7% and 92.5%.

</details>


### [50] [AVTrace: Diagnosing Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19991)
*Longyin Zhang,Parth Sakhare Mahendra,Chengwei Wei,Ning Zhang,Lim Ming Chong,Sirui He,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVTrace（音视频时序推理评估与能力评测），一个银标准诊断套件，覆盖事件起始/跨度定位、同步性、下一步预测、跨模态定位、链式解析及事件条件理解等任务。作者评估了五个开源 Omni 模型，发现它们在同步验证上均低于多数标签基线，且在链式解析与事件条件定位上表现不佳；参数高效时序后训练可改善部分指标，但外部图像基准出现部分退化。结论指出语义参考文本重叠不能作为时序定位能力的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Omni 模型能描述视频内容，但缺乏对其时序定位、事件顺序保持及音视频同步判断能力的系统评估，需要专门的诊断基准来揭示这些任务特定的薄弱环节。

Method: 构建银标准诊断套件 AVTrace，包含 34,114 条训练样本及类别平衡的 3,500 条验证集与 7,000 条测试集，覆盖七类任务。采用参考无关（reference-blind）的响应归一化后进行确定性评分；在各自输入配置下评估五个开源 Omni 模型，并通过验证集扰动实验考察模态移除与视觉输入处理变化的影响，同时进行参数高效时序后训练。

Result: 五个现成系统在同步验证任务上均低于测试集多数标签基线 0.556，链式解析与事件条件定位/理解得分也很低。扰动实验显示 Qwen3-Omni-30B 对模态移除和视觉处理变化存在任务依赖的敏感性。参数高效时序后训练提升了 Gemma4-E4B-it 在多项基准指标上的表现，但在三个外部图像基准上任务指标变化温和且含部分退化，教师强制困惑度下降。

Conclusion: 语义参考文本重叠不能作为时序定位能力的代理；AVTrace 能识别任务特定弱点，并为时序后训练提供测试平台。

Abstract: Omni models can describe video content, but can they locate events in time, preserve event order, and judge audio-visual synchronization? We introduce AVTrace (Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning Assessment and Capability Evaluation), a silver-standard diagnostic suite spanning onset and span grounding, synchronization, next-step prediction, cross-modal localization, chain parsing, and event-conditioned comprehension. It contains 34,114 training examples and category-balanced development and test splits of 3,500 and 7,000 examples. We evaluate five open omni models under their respective input configurations using reference-blind response normalization followed by deterministic scoring. All five off-the-shelf systems score below the test split's majority-label baseline of 0.556 on synchronization verification, and obtain low scores on chain parsing and event-conditioned grounding and comprehension. Development-set perturbations reveal task-dependent sensitivity in Qwen3-Omni-30B to modality removal and changes in visual input processing, without isolating their underlying causes. Parameter-efficient temporal post-training improves Gemma4-E4B-it on several benchmark metrics. On three external image benchmarks, task metrics change modestly, including some degradations, while teacher-forcing perplexity decreases. Together, these findings show that semantic reference-text overlap should not be treated as a proxy for temporal localization, and that AVTrace can identify task-specific weaknesses while providing a testbed for temporal post-training.

</details>


### [51] [GRF-Recon: Global Ray-Field Optimization for Long-Sequence Feed-forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20012)
*Enpeng Li,Yunzhou Zhang,Zhiyao Zhang,Dexuan Lyu,Chenyu Wang,Chiyuan Cui,Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向长单目序列的可扩展前馈三维重建统一框架，通过粗到细轨迹对齐、LoRA几何先验注入、混合权重稀疏光线场优化和联合光线误差轨迹拼接，在保持全局一致性与可扩展性的同时提升深度精度并抑制长期轨迹漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈三维重建在扩展到大尺度单目场景时受限于GPU显存占用过高、局部几何退化以及长期轨迹漂移；现有基于分块（chunk）的优化策略提供的几何约束有限，且难以在长轨迹上维持全局一致性。

Method: 构建统一框架：(1)粗到细的轨迹对齐，并注入轻量几何先验；(2)通过LoRA适配将单目几何线索蒸馏进前馈主干网络，在不牺牲推理效率的前提下提升细结构深度精度；(3)提出混合权重稀疏光线场优化，利用高频几何特征引导局部点云细化并施加一致的帧间光线约束，建立强跨帧几何耦合；(4)提出带联合光线误差优化的高效轨迹拼接策略，显式降低累积漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在大尺度场景下达到与代表性SLAM系统可竞争的轨迹精度，同时保持全局一致的三维重建。

Conclusion: 所提统一框架有效解决前馈三维重建在大尺度长单目序列中的显存、局部几何与轨迹漂移问题，在轨迹精度与全局重建一致性上均取得优异表现。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction provides an efficient paradigm for scene modeling from image sequences. Scaling these models to large monocular scenarios are constrained by excessive GPU memory footprint, degraded local geometry, and long-term trajectory drift. Existing chunk-based optimization strategies provide limited geometric constraints and fail to maintain global consistency over extended trajectories. We present a unified framework for stable and scalable feed-forward 3D reconstruction from long monocular sequences. Our approach builds on coarse-to-fine trajectory alignment augmented by lightweight geometric prior injection. Distilling monocular geometric cues into the feed-forward backbone via LoRA adaptation improves depth accuracy on fine structures while preserving inference efficiency. We introduce a hybrid-weight sparse ray-field optimization that leverages high-frequency geometric features to guide local point-cloud refinement and enforce consistent inter-frame ray constraints. Unlike prior chunk-based methods, this establishes strong cross-frame geometric coupling while maintaining scalability. Finally, an efficient trajectory stitching strategy with joint ray-error optimization explicitly reduces accumulated drift. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves competitive trajectory accuracy compared with representative SLAM systems, while maintaining globally consistent 3D reconstruction in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [52] [Astronex-World 1.0: Real-Time Interactive World Model Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20034)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Astronex-World 1.0，一个开源的、可控的视频世界模型基础模型，支持文本/图像到视频，在相机轨迹、连续动作和 embodiment 标识下预测未来视觉状态，并支持在 rollout 指定位置插入文本事件。该模型基于 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 先验，提供双向（全上下文生成）与因果（block-causal 注意力 + 跨块 KV 缓存，可持续生成）两类模型，通过五阶段训练实现 832x480@24fps 实时流式生成，在 WBench Navi/Full 上分别取得 73.5 / 70.0 分，性能超越或接近更大规模的同类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在可控性（相机轨迹、连续动作、交互式文本事件注入）、长时持续生成能力和计算效率之间存在权衡；作者希望构建一个开源、可实时流式运行、且具备完备控制接口的世界模型基础，为具身智能和自动驾驶等下游任务预留训练空间。

Method: 以 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 为先验，引入 PRoPE 注入相机内参/外参，通过 64 维动作流调制所有 Transformer 层；构建双向模型（全上下文生成）与因果模型（block-causal 注意力 + 跨块 KV 缓存实现持续生成）。采用五阶段训练路径：①发展双向相机与动作控制；②将骨干转换为 block-causal 生成；③蒸馏少步学生模型；④恢复混合域动态；⑤非对称 DMD/DMD2 分布匹配。全部训练在 2 块 NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU 上完成，因果模型可在单卡上实时流式生成 832x480@24fps。

Result: 因果模型生成 832x480@24fps 视频并可在单张 L20 上实时流式运行；评估得 WBench Navi 73.5 分、WBench Full 70.0 分。在 WBench Full 上，该 5B 模型超过 13.6B 的 LongCat-Video 与 14B 的 Helios，与 22B 的 LTX-2.3 差距在 1 分以内，并超过基于同一 5B 先验、在 A100 上后训练的 YUME 1.5。五阶段训练均仅用 2 块 L20 48GB GPU 完成。

Conclusion: Astronex-World 1.0 证明了一个 5B 规模的开源模型，通过精心设计的控制注入（PRoPE + 动作流 + embodiment 标识）、block-causal 因果架构和高效的五阶段训练，即可在低成本硬件上实现实时、可控的持续视频世界生成，性能达到或超越更大规模的基线。其预留的动作输入/输出接口为具身智能与自动驾驶的后续后训练提供了基础。

Abstract: We present Astronex-World 1.0, an open controllable video world-model foundation. Given a text prompt (text-to-video) or an initial observation (image-to-video), the model predicts future visual states under frame-aligned camera trajectories, continuous actions, and an embodiment identifier, and accepts text events inserted at a specified position of a rollout. The family provides a bidirectional model for full-context generation and a causal model with block-causal attention and cross-block KV caching for persistent generation, both built on the Wan2.2-TI2V-5B prior. PRoPE injects camera intrinsics and extrinsics, while a 64-dimensional action stream modulates every Transformer layer. A five-stage training path develops bidirectional camera and action control, converts the backbone to block-causal generation, distills a few-step student, restores mixed-domain dynamics, and applies asymmetric DMD/DMD2 distribution matching. The causal model generates 832x480 video at 24 fps. All five training stages run on two NVIDIA L20 48 GB GPUs, and the causal model streams in real time on one. It scores 73.5 on WBench Navi and 70.0 on WBench Full. On Full, this 5B model is above the 13.6B LongCat-Video and the 14B Helios, within one point of the 22B LTX-2.3, and above YUME 1.5, which is post-trained from the same 5B prior on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. The reserved action input and output interfaces allow post-training for embodied intelligence and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [53] [A Free Lunch? Adapting PP-OCRv6 for Historical Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20064)
*Benjamin Kiessling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了轻量级无循环文本识别器 PP-OCRv6 与传统 CRNN 在历史文本行识别中的表现。研究发现，从零训练时 PP-OCRv6 并不始终占优，但经过异构预训练后其泛化能力显著提升，微调后的 PP-OCRv6 甚至可超越面向历史拉丁文手写识别的大型视觉-语言模型 Medusa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型在历史自动文本识别（ATR）中尽管报告分数优异，但因计算成本高、依赖大规模预训练且存在幻觉问题，实际应用有限。历史 ATR 仍主要依赖紧凑的 CRNN 行识别器。轻量级无循环识别器有望兼顾大模型的精度与 CRNN 的实用优势，但尚未在历史书写数据上得到全面评估。

Method: 作者将近期紧凑型、无强语言建模的文本识别器 PP-OCRv6 适配于历史行识别，并与传统 CRNN 在以下四种场景下进行对比：广义预训练、领域特定训练、语料级微调以及手稿特定少样本适配，实验覆盖多语言拉丁文与阿拉伯文材料。此外，还与基于 Qwen3.5 的 Medusa 识别器（面向历史拉丁文手写文本识别的大规模视觉-语言模型）进行对照。

Result: 从零训练时 PP-OCRv6 并不始终优于 CRNN 基线；但经过异构预训练后，PP-OCRv6 表现出明显更好的泛化能力。与 Medusa 的对比显示，微调后的 PP-OCRv6 能够超越这一针对历史拉丁文手写文本识别定制的大型视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: 轻量级无循环识别器 PP-OCRv6 在合理预训练与微调条件下，可为历史文本识别提供兼具实用性与精度的可行方案，甚至在历史拉丁文手写识别上优于专用的大型视觉-语言模型，验证了其在历史 ATR 领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Despite impressive reported scores, large vision-language models have seen limited practical uptake in historical automatic text recognition because of their computational cost, dependence on large-scale pretraining, and hallucination. Historical ATR therefore continues to rely largely on compact CRNN line recognizers, which are visually grounded and trainable on modest data. Lightweight recurrence-free recognizers promise the accuracy of larger models with the practical advantages of CRNNs, yet have not been comprehensively evaluated on historical writing. We adapt PP-OCRv6, a recent compact text recognizer without strong language modeling, for historical line recognition and compare it with a conventional CRNN across generalized pretraining, domain-specific training, corpus-level fine-tuning, and manuscript-specific few-shot adaptation on multilingual Latin- and Arabic-script material. While PP-OCRv6 does not consistently outperform the baseline when trained from scratch, heterogeneous pretraining produces markedly better generalization. Comparisons with the Qwen3.5-based Medusa recognizer further show that fine-tuned PP-OCRv6 can outperform a large VLM tailored towards historical Latin-script HTR.

</details>


### [54] [PointEvent: Rethinking Event-based Tiny Object Detection via Serialized Motion Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20066)
*Zongze Wu,Baofeng Jia,Weiqi Yan,Jingyuan Zhang,Yu Zang,Xiaoyu Chen,Jing Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对远距离微小无人机检测中事件稀疏、碎片化且易被杂波和自运动干扰的问题，本文提出 PointEvent，一种轻量级事件级状态空间框架，通过串行化运动证据累积来建模跨远距离异步事件的运动连续性，在最少参数量和最快推理速度下达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机虽具备高时间分辨率与运动敏感性，但远距离目标产生的事件稀疏且碎片化，容易被杂波与相机自运动淹没。现有方法依赖密集事件表示或局部稀疏时空建模，分别导致计算冗余或无法完整建模远距离异步事件间的运动连续性。

Method: 提出串行化运动证据累积，将运动连续性视为有序的证据传播过程。将同一事件流通过潜在互补串行化组织为保持局部性的时空路径与保持时间顺序的时间路径，并据此设计 PointEvent——一个轻量级事件级状态空间框架，在互补顺序间交替串行扫描，突破固定局部邻域逐步整合碎片化运动证据。同时引入高分辨率事件分支保留细粒度目标响应，并通过紧凑的上下文调制抑制干扰。

Result: 实验表明，PointEvent 在对比方法中以最少的参数量和最快的实测推理速度达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过串行化运动证据累积有效建模远距离稀疏事件的运动连续性，PointEvent 兼顾精度与效率，为事件驱动的微小无人机检测提供了轻量高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and motion sensitivity for tiny UAV detection, yet distant targets generate sparse and fragmented events that are easily overwhelmed by clutter and ego-motion. Existing methods mainly rely on dense event representations or local sparse spatiotemporal modeling, resulting in redundant computation or fragmented modeling of motion continuity across distant asynchronous events. To address this limitation, we introduce serialized motion evidence accumulation, which treats motion continuity as an ordered evidence propagation process. Specifically, the same event stream is organized into locality-preserving spatiotemporal paths and chronology-preserving temporal paths through the latent complementary serializations. Based on this principle, we propose PointEvent, a lightweight event-wise state-space framework that alternates serialized scans across the complementary orders, progressively consolidating fragmented motion evidence beyond fixed local neighborhoods. A high-resolution event branch preserves fine-grained target responses, while compact context modulation suppresses interference. Experiments demonstrate that PointEvent achieves SOTA with the fewest parameters and fastest measured inference among the compared methods. Code: https://github.com/wzz-z/PointEvent

</details>


### [55] [G^2RA-NET: Graph-based Cross-Slice Relation Modeling with Attention Gating for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20088)
*Shengye Wang,Zonglin Wu,Liang Fan,Yule Xue,Haozhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 G^2RA-Net，一种结合图建模与注意力门控的医学图像分割框架，用于高效建模各向异性体数据中的跨切片关系，提升分割精度与边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割对定量临床分析和计算机辅助诊断至关重要。现有方法虽提升了局部特征表达与体素上下文建模，但在各向异性体图像中仍难以高效建模跨切片关系，导致分割一致性和准确性受限。

Method: 提出 G^2RA-Net，包含两个核心模块：1）基于图的切片关系建模（GSRM），将每个切片表示为图节点，通过图消息传递传播语义上下文以捕捉相邻切片间的解剖依赖；2）跨切片注意力门（CSAG），利用注意力引导的特征调制选择相关邻域上下文并突出目标解剖区域。

Result: 在脑 MRI 和腹部 CT 数据集上的实验表明，G^2RA-Net 在分割精度和边界质量上优于代表性方法，消融实验进一步验证了所提设计的有效性。

Conclusion: G^2RA-Net 通过图建模与注意力门控相结合的方式有效建模跨切片关系，显著提升各向异性医学体数据的分割性能，为医学图像分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation supports quantitative clinical analysis and computer-aided diagnosis. Recent methods for medical image segmentation have improved both local feature representation and volumetric context modeling. However, existing methods still strug- gle to efficiently model cross-slice relations in anisotropic volumet- ric images, limiting segmentation consistency and accuracy. This pa- per proposes G^2RA-Net, a medical image segmentation framework that combines graph-based cross-slice relation modeling with atten- tion gating. Graph-Based Slice Relationship Modeling (GSRM) cap- tures anatomical dependencies across consecutive slices by repre- senting each slice as a graph node and propagating semantic con- text through graph message passing. The Cross-Slice Attention Gate (CSAG) then selects relevant neighboring context and emphasizes target anatomical regions through attention-guided feature modula- tion. Experiments on brain MRI and abdominal CT datasets demon- strate that G^2RA-Net outperforms representative methods in seg- mentation accuracy and boundary quality. Ablation studies further validate the proposed design.

</details>


### [56] [A Smaller Transformer in Your Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20100)
*Dhananjay Tomar,Marius Aasan,Andreas Kleppe,Adín Ramírez Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Transformer-Within-Transformer（TWT），一种事后（post-hoc）方法，将 Vision Transformer 中连续冗余的层融合为单个可学习的替代层，从而降低参数量与推理计算量，并在自然图像及组织病理学下游任务中保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现 Vision Transformer 会收敛到局部相似的计算阶段，说明存在深度方向上的计算冗余。但现有利用这种冗余的方法，要么无法真正减少推理计算量，要么会严重损害模型表达能力。因此需要一种既能降低推理成本、又能保持模型性能的块冗余利用方法。

Method: 作者先形式化一个统一的块冗余视角，将几何结构（哪些是冗余层）与具体的替代干预手段解耦。在此基础上提出 Transformer-Within-Transformer（TWT），一种事后方法，把连续组的冗余层融合成一个单独的已学习替代层（surrogate layer），从而减少层数、参数和推理计算。

Result: TWT 在使用一半深度的情况下，在自然图像上与原始模型保持竞争力；在多个组织病理学下游任务中，TWT 与原始基线持平甚至有所提升，同时显著降低参数量和推理计算量。

Conclusion: 通过统一块冗余视角并采用 TWT 融合冗余层，可以在不牺牲（甚至提升）表达能力和下游性能的前提下有效降低 Vision Transformer 的推理成本，验证了深度方向冗余可被有效且无损地压缩利用。

Abstract: Recent findings indicate that Vision Transformers settle into locally similar computational phases, implying a level of depthwise computational redundancy. However, existing methods to exploit this redundancy either fail to reduce inference compute or severely degrade model expressivity. In this work, we formalise a unified view of block redundancy that decouples the geometry from specific surrogate interventions. We then introduce Transformer-Within-Transformer (TWT), a post-hoc method that fuses contiguous groups of redundant layers into a single learned surrogate layer. TWT reduces parameter count and inference compute while remaining competitive with original models using half the depth on natural images, and in several downstream histopathology settings, TWT matches or even improves on the original baseline.

</details>


### [57] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyviewMeter 是一个几何条件化的适配框架，通过低秩微调、Plücker 射线编码与同步块注意力，将相机几何信息和多视角证据融入预训练的机器人奖励模型，从而在视角变化与遮挡下提升任务进度预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型基于视觉观测评估任务执行，但其预测会随相机视角和遮挡改变，即使底层任务状态未变。因此，将预训练奖励模型适配到局部任务时，必须考虑任务如何被观测，需要引入相机几何与多视角联合证据来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 AnyviewMeter 框架，将任务进度表示为标量奖励信号。它结合低秩微调（LoRA）、与 token 对齐的 Plücker 射线编码以及同步块注意力：射线条件化将相机几何融入视觉特征和注意力的查询与键，块注意力则在预训练解码器内融合同步视角。框架支持单视角奖励预测和联合多视角评估，并通过参数高效的方式适配预训练的 Robometer 模型。

Result: 在 PickCube 上，单视角适配在每组相机下均提升进度预测，且在改变视野时相比 RGB 微调将平均绝对误差降低约 21%。在仿真操作任务中，联合多视角预测相比单视角 RGB 预测平均值将进度误差降低 41-69%，并在约 88% 的任务-相机组中改善时序排序。在固定相机和腕部相机的真实任务中，相比 RGB 微调平均值，平均绝对误差降低约 21%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，相机几何和联合视觉证据是任务特定机器人奖励适配的有用组成部分，AnyviewMeter 能有效提升跨视角和遮挡条件下的奖励预测鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: Robotic reward models evaluate task execution from visual observations, but their predictions can change with camera viewpoint and occlusion even when the underlying task state is unchanged. Adapting a pretrained reward model to a local task therefore requires accounting for how that task is observed. We introduce AnyviewMeter, a geometry-conditioned adaptation framework for robotic reward models that represent task progress as a scalar reward signal. It combines low-rank fine-tuning with token-aligned Plucker rays and synchronous block attention: ray conditioning incorporates camera geometry into visual features and attention queries and keys, while block attention fuses synchronized views inside the pretrained decoder. The framework supports both single-view reward prediction and joint multi-view evaluation through parameter-efficient adaptation of a pretrained Robometer model. On PickCube, single-view adaptation improves progress prediction in every camera group and reduces mean absolute error under a changed field of view by approximately 21% relative to RGB fine-tuning. Across simulated manipulation tasks, joint multi-view prediction reduces progress error by 41-69% compared with averaging single-view RGB predictions and improves temporal ordering in approximately 88% of task-camera groups. On real tasks with fixed and wrist-mounted cameras, mean absolute error decreases by approximately 21% relative to averaged RGB fine-tuning. These results support camera geometry and joint visual evidence as useful components of task-specific robotic reward adaptation.

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Modal Attention Acts as a Frequency Filter: Why Verbose Prompts Improve Robustness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20139)
*Farooq Ahmad Wani,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Mujtaba Hussain Mirza,Oleksandr Pryymak,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Iacopo Masi,Pasquale Minervini,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示视觉语言模型对图像损坏的脆弱性受问题措辞双向影响：冗长问题提升鲁棒性，语义细粒度问题加剧脆弱性；二者均源于问题引导的跨模态注意力形成的光谱滤波器，冗长措辞拓宽其频域支撑、细粒度问题收缩其视觉尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在图像损坏下表现脆弱，而问题的具体措辞如何影响这种脆弱性尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示问题措辞对 VLM 鲁棒性的两种相反作用机制，并提供可操作的缓解策略。

Method: 作者将问题措辞的影响归因于"问题条件化跨模态注意力"所诱导的图像块光谱滤波器：冗长问题拓宽滤波器的频率支撑，细粒度问题将注意力集中在更少的视觉尺度上。通过在 Qwen3-VL 和 LLaVA-OneVision 上、于 GQA 和 CLEVR 数据集验证该滤波器观点，并提出"填充提示词"的实用策略。

Result: 冗长改写使 8B 模型的漂移方差降低 70–81%；"填充提示词"策略即使在图像损坏下也带来可测量的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 问题措辞通过问题条件化跨模态注意力的光谱滤波效应双向调节 VLM 鲁棒性：冗长措辞拓宽频域支撑增强鲁棒性，细粒度措辞收缩注意力加剧脆弱性；该机制可转化为实用的提示词填充策略以提升损坏图像下的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are fragile under image corruption. We find that the wording of the question affects VLMs in two opposite ways. Verbose questions make VLMs substantially more robust---e.g., rephrasing "Is there a cat?" into "Please look carefully and answer: is there a cat?". Conversely, VLMs become more fragile under corruption when the question is semantically complex or finer-grained, e.g., "what colour is the cup left of the chair?" instead of "is there a cup?". Both effects stem from question-conditioned cross-modal attention, which induces a spectral filter over image patches: verbose questions broaden its frequency support, while fine-grained questions concentrate it onto fewer visual scales. The model's answer drifts most when this filter and the corruption sit on the same spatial frequencies. We test the filter view on Qwen3-VL and LLaVA-OneVision across GQA and CLEVR; verbose paraphrasing reduces drift variance by 70--81% on the 8B models. The practical recipe---pad the prompt---further yields measurable gains in accuracy, even under image corruption.

</details>


### [59] [Bridging Modalities on the Cortex: Surface-based MRI to PET Translation with a Diffusion Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20147)
*Yitong Li,Alexandra Samoylova,Fabian Bongratz,Timo Grimmer,Dennis M. Hedderich,Igor Yakushev,Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于皮层表面的扩散桥框架 DB-SUiT，利用条件球形U型视觉Transformer（SUiT）从MRI合成FDG-PET，以低成本、无辐射方式替代真实PET用于痴呆诊断，在合成保真度与诊断性能上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDG-PET检测的皮层低代谢是痴呆诊断的高敏感生物标志物，但成本高、有辐射暴露且可及性受限；现有从MRI跨模态合成的方法多为体素级生成，未显式考虑疾病模式主要分布的高度折叠的皮层几何结构，因此需要在皮层流形上原生地进行MRI到PET的转换。

Method: 提出表面扩散桥框架 DB-SUiT，在皮层流形上原生实现MRI到PET翻译。核心是条件球形U型视觉Transformer（SUiT）：结合球面卷积编码器进行多尺度表面特征提取，以瓶颈Transformer捕捉长程空间依赖，保持表面拓扑，并融入人口统计学和皮层下条件信息以细化合成结果。模型在两个数据集上评估，并在未重训练的外部队列（含训练未见过的痴呆亚型）上测试跨队列、跨病理泛化能力。

Result: 在合成保真度上，DB-SUiT 大幅优于其他基线。自动痴呆分类中，合成PET表面相比MRI提升14.2%、相比PET体积提升11.3%，接近真实PET表面的性能。盲法阅片研究中，合成PET诊断准确率达85.5%，而MRI为75.8%、真实PET为95.2%。模型在未重训练的情况下对包含训练期未见痴呆亚型的外部队列仍表现良好，证明其跨队列与跨病理泛化性。

Conclusion: DB-SUiT通过在皮层表面上原生建模，成功实现高保真的MRI到PET合成，显著提升痴呆诊断性能并具备良好的跨队列、跨病理泛化能力，为临床提供了一种低成本、无辐射的PET替代方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Cortical hypometabolism measured by Fluorodeoxyglucose Positron Emission Tomography (FDG-PET) is a highly sensitive biomarker for dementia diagnosis. However, high costs, radiation exposure, and limited accessibility constrain its clinical utility. While cross-modal synthesis from Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) offers a promising alternative, existing volumetric generation methods do not explicitly account for the highly folded cortical geometry, where disease-related patterns predominantly reside. To address this, we introduce a novel surface-based diffusion bridge framework DB-SUiT for MRI-to-PET translation that operates natively on the cortical manifold. A conditional Spherical U-shaped vision Transformer (SUiT) is specifically designed to model the intricate cross-modal relationships while preserving surface topology. It combines spherical convolutional encoders for multi-scale surface feature extraction with bottleneck Transformers to capture long-range spatial dependencies, while incorporating demographic and subcortical conditions to refine the synthesis. Evaluated on two datasets, including subjects with different dementia types, DB-SUiT demonstrates high-fidelity synthesis that substantially outperforms other baselines. In automated dementia classification, synthesized PET surfaces improve performance over MRI by 14.2% and PET volumes by 11.3%, approaching the performance of real PET surfaces. In a blinded reader study, synthetic PET achieved 85.5% diagnostic accuracy, compared with 75.8% for MRI and 95.2% for real PET. This further demonstrates cross-cohort and cross-pathology generalization, as the model was evaluated without retraining on an external cohort that included a dementia subtype not represented during training. Our code is available at https://github.com/ai-med/DB-SUiT.

</details>


### [60] [Task-Oriented Semantic Feature Transmission for Multi-Task Satellite Remote Sensing over Low-SNR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20150)
*Shuoyuan Sun,Hongyu Wang,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务导向的卫星遥感语义通信框架，跳过图像重建，直接传输多任务预训练骨干网络提取的语义特征，通过轻量信道适配模块与特征恢复器联合优化，在低信噪比下显著优于传统重建导向的联合信源信道编码（JSCC）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星遥感传输遵循"先重建再推理"的范式，优化目标是像素级保真度，这与分类、检测等下游任务目标不匹配，尤其在低信噪比条件下问题突出。因此需要一种以任务为导向、绕过重建环节的语义传输框架。

Method: 采用多任务预训练骨干网络直接提取语义特征并传输，而非传输重建图像。引入轻量信道适配模块（CAM）压缩特征维度以降低带宽，并用特征恢复器在信道损伤后恢复任务相关结构。骨干网络冻结，在随机信噪比训练下，联合优化CAM与任务专用下游头，采用任务级与特征级双重监督。

Result: 在加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）设定下，场景分类与目标检测实验表明，本方法在不同信噪比条件下均一致优于重建导向的JSCC基线，且在低信噪比区间增益最大。

Conclusion: 任务导向的语义特征直接传输框架能有效弥合传输优化目标与下游任务之间的错配，尤其在低信噪比卫星遥感场景中具备显著优势。

Abstract: Conventional satellite remote sensing transmission follows a reconstruct-then-infer paradigm that optimizes pixel-level fidelity, creating an objective mismatch with downstream tasks such as classification and detection, especially at low SNR. This paper investigates a task-oriented framework that bypasses image reconstruction and directly transmits semantic features extracted by a multitask-pretrained backbone. A lightweight channel adaptation module (CAM) compresses feature dimensionality for bandwidth reduction, and a feature restorer recovers task-relevant structure after channel corruption. With the backbone frozen, the CAM and task-specific downstream heads are jointly optimized with task and feature-level supervision under random-SNR training. Under the adopted AWGN setting, experiments on scene classification and object detection show consistent gains over reconstruction-oriented JSCC baselines across different SNR conditions, with the largest improvements in the low-SNR regime.

</details>


### [61] [Ischemic Stroke Segmentation and Net Water Uptake Quantification on Multicenter Non-Contrast CT Using Supervised Target-Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20151)
*Linus Britt,Maximilian Nielsen,Susan Klapproth,Andre Kemmling,Michael H. Lev,Gabriel Broocks,Rene Werner,Thilo Sentker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并外部验证了一种基于nnU-Net的域感知深度学习框架，用于在非对比CT（NCCT）上自动分割缺血性卒中病灶，并评估其用于净水摄取量（NWU）定量的可行性。通过在目标域小样本上微调，模型在异质性多中心数据上实现了可用的分割效果，并支持仅凭NCCT即可进行低误差的NWU量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCCT梗死低密度区的定量评估（如NWU）目前依赖人工或半自动病灶勾画，且常需CT灌注或DWI-MRI引导，临床应用受限。自动分割可高效提取NWU等生物标志物，但在异质性多中心数据上仍具挑战，因此需要一种能跨域适应的自动NCCT分割框架。

Method: 回顾性多中心研究，纳入4个数据集共801例患者。采用基于nnU-Net的模型，在汉堡-埃彭多夫大学医学中心和急性缺血性卒中数据集（AISD）的NCCT上训练；对来自Boston（n=11）和ISLES（n=75）的目标域子集进行微调以适应新域，并在未用于微调的留出病例上进行评估；将自动分割结果和NWU值与专家参考标准进行对比。

Result: 对于≥30 mL的病灶，中位Dice系数为0.68（Boston）和0.56（ISLES）；纳入以ISLES为主的小病灶后，Boston数据集急性病灶分割中位Dice为0.54（IQR 0.30–0.70），而ISLES挑战主要目标（治疗后梗死灶）的NCCT病灶分割中位Dice为0.20（IQR 0.03–0.41）。Boston数据集的自动化NWU平均绝对误差为1.37个百分点（SD 1.61）。

Conclusion: 目标域适应支持了跨异质性外部队列的NCCT-only梗死分割，尽管性能因域而异。该方法实现了无需高级影像即可从基线NCCT获得低误差的NWU量化，支持进一步的前瞻性临床评估。

Abstract: Objectives: Quantitative assessment of infarct hypodensity on non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT), including net water uptake (NWU), requires manual or semi-manual lesion delineation, often guided by CT perfusion or diffusion-weighted MRI, limiting clinical applicability. Automated segmentation on NCCT could enable efficient biomarker extraction such as NWU but remains challenging across heterogeneous multicenter data. This study aimed to develop and externally test a domain-aware deep learning framework for ischemic stroke segmentation on NCCT and assess its suitability for NWU quantification.
  Materials & Methods: In this retrospective multicenter study of 801 patients from four datasets, an nnU-Net-based model was trained on NCCT scans from the University Medical Center Hamburg-Eppendorf and the Acute Ischemic Stroke Dataset. To adapt to new domains, the model was fine-tuned on target-domain subsets from Boston (n=11) and ISLES (n=75), with evaluation on held-out cases not used for fine-tuning. Automated segmentations and NWU values were compared with expert references.
  Results: For lesions $\geq$ 30 mL, median Dice was 0.68 (Boston) and 0.56 (ISLES). Including smaller lesions, which predominated in ISLES, median Dice was 0.54 (interquartile range [IQR] 0.30-0.70) for acute lesion segmentation (Boston dataset) and 0.20 (IQR 0.03-0.41) for NCCT lesion segmentations when compared to post-treatment infarct (primary target of the ISLES challenge). Automated NWU mean absolute error was 1.37 percentage points (SD 1.61, Boston).
  Conclusion: Target-domain adaptation supported NCCT-only infarct segmentation across heterogeneous external cohorts, although performance varied across domains. The approach enabled low-error NWU quantification from baseline NCCT without advanced imaging, supporting further prospective clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [62] [Needles in a Raystack: Ultra-Sparse LiDAR Occupancy Detection for Bat Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20160)
*Nico Klar,Pankaj Rana,Nizam Gifary,Jakob Traub,Aamir Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用 LiDAR 检测夜间蝙蝠的飞行轨迹。作者将问题建模为传感器中心 LiDAR 射线堆叠中的体素占用检测，提出一种轻量级 3D U-Net，结合加权二元交叉熵与 Dice 损失处理极端类别不平衡，成功恢复蝙蝠飞行的稀疏前景占用，而重建式 3D 卷积自编码器基线则失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测飞行动物对理解和保护生物多样性很重要，但蝙蝠等夜行性物种难以在野外观察。LiDAR 虽能记录夜间蝙蝠运动，却产生超稀疏的 3D 时空数据，标准重建损失往往只预测背景而漏掉真实飞行路径，因此需要针对性的检测方法。

Method: 将蝙蝠检测建模为传感器中心 LiDAR 射线堆叠（raystack）中的体素级占用检测。提出轻量级 3D U-Net：①保持时间分辨率；②利用跳跃连接保留空间细节；③组合加权二元交叉熵与 Dice 损失应对强类别不平衡。并与基于重建的 3D 卷积自编码器基线对比。

Result: 在开阔农田的真实 LiDAR 蝙蝠记录上（以声学监测交叉验证）：重建式 3D 卷积自编码器基线无法恢复前景轨迹；所提 U-Net 在诊断实验中恢复稀疏前景占用，并沿蝙蝠飞行轨迹产生连贯的占用模式。

Conclusion: 该方法为验证规模的实验、后续飞行轨迹聚类，以及未来将蝙蝠活动信息融入生物多样性感知的风机限功率（curtailment）策略提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Monitoring flying animals is important for understanding and protecting biodiversity, but nocturnal species such as bats are difficult to observe in the field. Using LiDAR, bat movements at night result in ultra-sparse 3D spatio-temporal data in which standard reconstruction losses tend to predict only background and miss real flight paths. We study this problem as voxel-wise occupancy detection in sensor-centric LiDAR raystacks. A lightweight 3D U-Net is proposed that preserves temporal resolution, uses skip connections for spatial detail, and combines weighted binary cross-entropy with Dice loss to handle the strong class imbalance.
  In real LiDAR recordings of bats over open fields, cross-checked with acoustic monitoring, a reconstruction-based 3D convolutional autoencoder baseline fails to recover foreground trajectories. In contrast, the proposed U-Net recovers sparse foreground occupancy in diagnostic experiments and produces coherent occupancy patterns along bat flight trajectories, providing a practical basis for validation-scale experiments, later clustering of flight tracks, and future integration of bat activity information into biodiversity-aware turbine curtailment strategies.

</details>


### [63] [MTF-Net: Multi-Modal Temporal Feature Fusion Network for Pedestrian Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20178)
*Md Mahfuzur Rahman,Pengzhan Zhou,A. F. M. Abdun Noor,Md Imam Ahasan,Md Mustafizur Rahman,Fang Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MTF-Net 是一个多模态时序特征融合网络，通过门控线性单元自适应融合边界框动态、人体姿态关键点、局部上下文和场景语义四种模态，在 PIE 和 JAAD 数据集上分别达到 0.95 和 0.94 AUC，超越近期 transformer 和图模型，并保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测行人意图对自动驾驶车辆与行人的安全主动交互至关重要。现有方法或只建模单一模态内的时序依赖，或仅在粗粒度语义层面融合多模态，导致意图预测不准。

Method: 提出 MTF-Net 多模态时序特征融合网络，整合四种互补模态（边界框动态、人体姿态关键点、局部上下文、场景级语义），在增强门控线性单元（GLU）的循环融合框架中建模；GLU 模块自适应调节跨模态信息流，实现可解释且高效的时空特征交互；通过三个专用时序编码分支和注意力引导融合头，在事件发生前数帧提前预测行人过街意图。

Result: 在 PIE 和 JAAD 基准上的大量实验表明，MTF-Net 超越近期 transformer 和图模型，PIE 上 AUC 达 0.95，JAAD 上 AUC 达 0.94，同时保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的行人意图预测源于有原则的多模态融合，而非过度的架构复杂性；MTF-Net 证明了门控融合机制在精度和实时性上的有效性。

Abstract: Accurately predicting pedestrian intentions is crucial for ensuring safe and proactive interaction between autonomous vehicles and pedestrians. However, existing approaches often depend on architectures that either model temporal dependencies within individual modalities or fuse modalities only at coarse semantic levels. To address these limitations, we propose MTF-Net, a novel Multi-Modal Temporal Feature Fusion Network that jointly models kinematic, appearance, and contextual cues for pedestrian intention prediction. MTF-Net integrates four complementary modalities-bounding-box dynamics, human pose keypoints, local context, and scene-level semantics within a recurrent fusion framework enhanced by gated linear units (GLUs). These GLU-based modules adaptively regulate cross-modal information flow, enabling interpretable and efficient feature interaction across temporal scales. Through three dedicated temporal encoding branches and an attention-guided fusion head, the proposed model robustly anticipates pedestrian crossing intentions several frames before they occur. Extensive evaluations on the PIE and JAAD benchmarks demonstrate that MTF-Net surpasses recent transformer- and graph-based models, achieving up to 0.95 AUC on PIE and 0.94 AUC on JAAD, while maintaining real-time performance. The results highlight that reliable pedestrian intention prediction arises from principled multi-modal fusion rather than excessive architectural complexity.

</details>


### [64] [MoSSGate: Memory-Modulated State-Space Gating for Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20181)
*Anum Awan,Mahnoor Buriro,Muhammad Younas Khan,Md Imam Ahasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoSSGate模块，通过边界感知空间门控、外部记忆调制器与并行2D状态空间建模，使U-Net在有限算力下同时兼顾全局上下文与精细边界，实现高效精准的皮肤病变分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积与Transformer模型在有限计算预算下难以兼顾长程空间依赖与精细边界细节，常导致过度分割、碎片化预测或丢失细小周边结构，需要在全局建模与边界定位之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出即插即用的MoSSGate模块集成入U-Net，包含三部分：(i) 边界感知空间门控，将长程传播限制在信息丰富区域；(ii) 外部记忆调制器，提供样本自适应的动态控制；(iii) 并行2D状态空间建模，以线性复杂度高效聚合全局上下文。

Result: 在ISIC 2017与ISIC 2018基准上取得领先精度：mIoU分别为86.3%和85.9%，Dice分别为92.6%和90.6%，且FLOPs显著低于多数竞争性CNN方法，展现出良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: MoSSGate通过自适应、上下文感知的信息传播，在保持清晰准确病变边界的同时实现高效全局建模，为高分辨率医学图像分割提供了有利的精度-效率平衡方案。

Abstract: Accurate skin lesion segmentation is crucial for reliable computer-aided dermatological diagnosis, yet existing convolutional and transformer-based models often struggle to jointly capture long-range spatial dependencies and fine boundary details under limited computational budgets. This trade-off between global context modeling and boundary-aware localization frequently leads to over-segmentation, fragmented predictions, or missing thin peripheral structures. To address this challenge, we propose MoSSGate, a plug-and-play module for U-Net that integrates (i) boundary-aware spatial gating to restrict long-range propagation to informative regions, (ii) an external memory modulator that provides sample-adaptive dynamic control, and (iii) parallel 2D state-space modeling for efficient global context aggregation with linear complexity. The proposed design enables adaptive, context-aware information propagation while preserving sharp and accurate lesion boundaries. Extensive experiments on the ISIC 2017 and ISIC 2018 benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy with strong efficiency, achieving 86.3% and 85.9% mIoU and 92.6% and 90.6% Dice, respectively, while requiring substantially fewer FLOPs than most competing CNN-based methods. These results highlight a favorable accuracy efficiency trade-off for high-resolution medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [65] [A Two-Stage Multi-Scale Attention-Based Network for Weakly Supervised Cataract Fundus Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20222)
*Xiaoyong Fang,Yue Wang,Xiangyu Li,Wanshu Fan,Dongsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段多尺度注意力网络（TSMSA-Net），用于弱监督的白内障眼底图像增强。该方法利用域变换合成逼真的成对白内障图像以解决配对数据难以获取的问题，并通过多尺度注意力阶段提取细节、抑制伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白内障是导致视力丧失的主要原因之一，并妨碍进一步诊断。白内障眼底图像增强面临两大挑战：一是成对的白内障视网膜图像数量有限，难以获取；二是对视网膜图像中精细细节的恢复不足。

Method: 提出两阶段多尺度注意力网络（TSMSA-Net）：第一阶段利用域变换合成逼真的成对白内障图像，从而解决配对图像难以获取的问题；第二阶段采用多尺度注意力机制学习更有用的细节特征，以减少增强过程中的伪影并提升细节恢复能力。整体采用弱监督学习方式。

Result: 在 Kaggle 和 ODIR-5K 数据集上的实验表明，TSMSA-Net 在没有成对图像的情况下仍优于当前最先进的白内障眼底图像增强方法，并展现出一定的泛化能力；同时，增强结果还能提升白内障图像中血管分割和分类任务的性能。

Conclusion: TSMSA-Net 有效解决了白内障眼底图像增强中配对数据不足和细节恢复不佳的问题，在无成对图像条件下取得领先性能并具备泛化能力，且对下游血管分割与分类任务具有促进作用。

Abstract: Cataract is a major cause of vision loss and hinders further diagnosis. However, cataract fundus image enhancement often grapples with challenges such as limited paired cataract retinal images and insufficient recovery of fine details in the retinal images. To mitigate these challenges, we in this paper propose a two-stage multi-scale attention-based network (TSMSA-Net) for weakly supervised cataract fundus image enhancement. Our TSMSA-Net leverages the domain transformation to synthesis paired real-like cataract images, solving the problem of difficult acquisition of paired images. To further extract detailed information from fundus images and reduce the generation of artifacts during the enhancement process, we propose a multi-scale attention-based stage to learn more useful features for cataract image enhancement. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K demonstrate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms current state-of-the-art cataract fundus images enhancement even without paired images and exhibits certain generalization ability. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K datasets indicate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms the current state-of-the-art methods for cataract fundus image enhancement, even in the absence of paired images. Additionally, it demonstrates a certain level of generalization capability. The enhancement also can improve the performance of vessel segmentation and classification in cataract images.

</details>


### [66] [Scene-Q: Confidence-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Querying of 3D Scenes with Selective VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20235)
*Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Hye-Jung Yoon,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scene-Q 是一个置信度感知的由粗到细查询框架：用温度缩放归一化对比编码器分数，仅对低置信度查询调用推理型 VLM 进行基于多视角图像与实例框的上下文重排序，从而在低成本下实现开放词汇 3D 场景理解与自然语言实例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内移动机器人需要将自然语言查询定位到一致的 3D 地图中。现有系统大多依赖对比式图文编码器的余弦相似度检索，虽高效但在标签近义或多相似实例并存时表现脆弱、易混淆。

Method: 提出 Scene-Q 框架：(1) 用温度缩放对编码器分数做归一化（confidence-aware）；(2) 高置信度查询直接走快速检索路径；(3) 低置信度查询仅在 top-K 候选中，利用原始多视图图像与实例包围框调用推理型 VLM 重新排序，实现上下文感知的消歧。

Result: 在 ScanNet200 上提升开放词汇 3D 实例分割，在真实世界重建上提升自然语言 3D 实例检索；在空间与关系类查询上提升最大，同时保持相当比例查询走快速路径。

Conclusion: Scene-Q 通过温度缩放归一化置信度并在模糊情况下才启用推理型 VLM，兼顾了效率与鲁棒性，显著改善开放词汇 3D 实例分割与自然语言检索性能，尤其在空间和关系查询上收益明显。

Abstract: Indoor mobile robots require open-vocabulary scene understanding that grounds natural-language queries in a consistent 3D map. Many existing systems ultimately rely on cosine-similarity retrieval with contrastive image--text encoders, which is efficient but brittle when labels are near-synonymous or multiple similar instances appear. We present Scene-Q, a confidence-aware coarse-to-fine querying framework that normalizes encoder scores with temperature scaling and selectively invokes a reasoning VLM only for low-confidence cases. High-confidence queries are answered by fast retrieval, while ambiguous ones are reranked over a small top-K candidate set using the original multi-view images and instance bounding boxes, enabling context-aware disambiguation at low cost.
