Yet full-attention decoding reads the growing history at every step, regardless of its benefit to the next prediction. We show that a pretrained model's decoding states already contain information predictive of this benefit, before the global read. Building on this finding, we introduce On-Demand Attention (ODA), a local-first decoding method that uses a lightweight recall head to selectively invoke global attention as its predicted benefit changes during generation. ODA trains only the recall head, leaving pretrained weights unchanged and the complete historical KV cache available for future recall. We further implement GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, translating reduced global reads into practical decoding speedups over full attention at long context lengths. Experiments across Qwen and Gemma models, including hybrid-attention backbones, show that selective recall recovers most of the performance lost under local attention while substantially reducing global reads. These findings support long-context inference in which pretrained models guide their own access to the information they retain.

</details>


### [168] [dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751)
*Anton Xue,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Adam Klivans,Sujay Sanghavi,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将混合架构（注意力与RNN层交错）的自回归模型 Qwen3.5 自适应为扩散语言模型（DLM），提出 dQwen3.5 系列，发现混合骨干网络作为 DLM 的起点效率更高（达到相同训练损失仅需约一半 token），且在任意顺序解码和并行解码上表现与全注意力 DLM 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练自回归（AR）模型自适应为扩散语言模型（DLM）成本低，但现有工作几乎都从全注意力 Transformer 出发。而当前 AR 建模已转向注意力与 RNN 层交错的混合架构，RNN 结构上具有因果性、难以双向化，这为自适应带来障碍。作者希望探究这类混合骨干能否被有效改造为 DLM。

Method: 以 Qwen3.5 的 0.8B、2B、4B、9B 四个规模为骨干，将其自适应为扩散语言模型，得到 dQwen3.5 系列；并与全注意力控制模型对比训练效率，考察任意顺序解码（any-order decoding）行为及并行解码（parallel decoding）下的性能。

Result: 混合骨干可成为高效的自适应起点：相比全注意力对照模型，混合架构仅需约一半的 token 即可达到相同训练损失；在各规模上，dQwen3.5 的任意顺序解码行为与全注意力 DLM 相似，并在并行解码下表现强劲。

Conclusion: 尽管 RNN 结构上因果且难以双向化，混合注意力-RNN 骨干仍可被成功自适应为高效且性能强的扩散语言模型，为低成本构建 DLM 提供了新路径。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). While nearly all such adaptations start from a full-attention transformer, AR modeling has shifted toward hybrid architectures that interleave attention and RNN layers. This creates an obstacle for adaptation: unlike attention, RNNs are structurally causal and nontrivial to bidirectionalize. Despite this mismatch, we investigate whether such backbones can become effective DLMs by adapting Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, yielding the dQwen3.5 family. We find that hybrid backbones can be efficient starting points for adaptation: against a full-attention control, the hybrid reaches a given training loss in about half the tokens. Across scales, dQwen3.5 resembles full-attention DLMs in any-order decoding behavior and performs strongly under parallel decoding.

</details>


### [169] [Harm Laundering in GPT Models: Evidence That Gender Discrimination Is Transformed Rather Than Reduced Across Safety-Trained Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20779)
*Sarah Wyer,Sue Black,Noura Al Moubayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型安全评估所依赖的表层分类器其毒性评分随模型代际下降，但这并未真正减少危害，而是将显式歧视内容"转化"为更隐蔽的偏见——作者称之为"危害洗白"（harm laundering）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估依赖表层形式分类器，毒性/伤害评分随模型更新持续下降，造成"模型越来越安全"的假象。作者质疑这一方法学是否系统性地不完整，即显式歧视内容是否只是被转化而非被真正移除。

Method: 在OpenAI GPT谱系（GPT-2至GPT-5）的15个模型上，对三种人口学条件下的45万条性别定向补全文本进行主题聚类与量化分析。结合三个独立毒性分类器评分、情感分数、主题多样性（W/M比值）、以及REGARD与Detoxify两类指标，考察毒性评分与表征性伤害随发布时间及对齐边界的变化，并据此形式化"危害洗白"的三条判别标准与三阶段检测协议。

Result: GPT-2中女性定向输出常见的性暴力主题簇在GPT-4时消失，而男性定向补全获得了女性定向所没有的积极表征空间（照料、情感表达、盟友身份）；GPT-5中Topic 5（1997篇文档）将乳腺癌框架为"男性权利"议题，而女性定向输出中无对等簇。三个独立分类器将该内容判为非毒性。情感分数在GPT-4发生反转：早期模型贬低女性，后期模型过度纠正。女性定向补全的主题多样性在GPT-4对齐边界处相对男性下降36%（W/M从GPT-2的0.91降至0.58）。REGARD表征性伤害差异与发布日期显著正相关（ρ=+0.55，p=.034），而Detoxify毒性评分无此相关性（ρ=-0.23，p=.42），表明毒性评分下降的同时表征性伤害在上升。

Conclusion: 在OpenAI GPT谱系内，毒性评分的下降并不能充分代理危害的下降。显式歧视内容被转化为更隐蔽的表征性伤害，需要采用三标准检验与三阶段检测协议来识别"危害洗白"，以补足表层分类器评估的不足。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models rely on surface-form classifiers that report declining harm scores across model generations. We provide evidence that this methodology is systematically incomplete: explicit discriminatory content is transformed rather than removed. We call this \emph{harm laundering}. Analysing 450,000 gender-directed completions across 15 models spanning GPT-2 through to GPT-5 (OpenAI GPT lineage; three demographic conditions), we show that sexual violence clusters prevalent in GPT-2 women-directed output disappear by GPT-4, while men-directed completions gain positive representational territory (caregiving, emotional range, ally identity) that women-directed completions do not. The pattern is most visible at GPT-5: Topic~5 (1,997~documents) frames breast cancer as a men's rights debate, while zero equivalent clusters appear in women-directed output. Three independent classifiers score this content as non-toxic. Sentiment scores invert at GPT-4: early models demean women; later models over-correct. Topic diversity in women-directed completions falls 36\% relative to men at the GPT-4 alignment boundary (W/M~$= 0.58$, from $0.91$ at GPT-2). REGARD representational harm disparity correlates with release date ($ρ= +0.55$, $p = .034$) while Detoxify does not ($ρ= -0.23$, $p = .42$): toxicity scores fall as representational harm grows. We formalise harm laundering as a three-criteria test and provide a three-stage detection protocol applicable to any generative model. Within the OpenAI GPT lineage, toxicity score reduction is not a sufficient proxy for harm reduction.

</details>


### [170] [RetireOPD: Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20784)
*Yan Yu,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Yichen Pan,Aozhe Wang,Qipeng Chen,Hua Yang,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RetireOPD（自退休在线策略蒸馏）方法，通过解耦的技能条件教师、联合 RL 与 OPD 训练，并采用自适应退休策略，让技能无关的学生在蒸馏收敛后自行停止依赖教师，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上显著超越 RL 基线及教师本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体（agentic）任务中，现有的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）存在两个关键问题：一是仅有特权信息并不能保证教师可靠；二是教师监督的收益具有阶段依赖性，在训练的某些阶段反而可能妨碍学习。因此需要一种能判断何时停止依赖教师的方法。

Method: RetireOPD 分两步：1) 先用环境奖励优化一个解耦的、以技能为条件的教师（skill-conditioned teacher）；2) 再联合强化学习（RL）与 OPD 训练一个技能无关的学生。关键创新是"自适应退休"（Adaptive Retirement）：不再遵循预设的蒸馏时间表，而是当学生与教师之间的差异（discrepancy）不再缩小，且学生成功率达到教师成功率的某个目标比例时，学生自行停止使用教师监督，后续仅用 RL 继续训练。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 系列模型（1.5B 至 7B）上，RetireOPD 使 ALFWorld 任务成功率较 RL 基线提升 14.1% 至 18.8%，WebShop 准确率提升 11.8% 至 19.0%，并且在所有设置下都超越了其自身的技能条件教师。

Conclusion: RetireOPD 通过解耦教师优化与自适应退休机制，有效解决了教师不可靠和蒸馏收益阶段依赖的问题，在不依赖外部预定义时间表的情况下实现了稳定且显著的性能提升，展示了学生自主判断退出时机这一思路在智能体蒸馏中的价值。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) receive a single scalar reward per trajectory, which motivates self on-policy distillation (OPD) to supply dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher with privileged task skills, letting a skill-free student internalize them. This recipe, however, is undermined by two findings in agentic tasks: privileged information alone does not always make a teacher reliable, and the benefit of teacher supervision is stage-dependent. We therefore propose RetireOPD (Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation), which first optimizes a decoupled, skill-conditioned teacher with environment rewards and then trains a skill-free student jointly with RL and OPD. Rather than following a predefined distillation schedule, RetireOPD adopts Adaptive Retirement: the student drops the teacher on its own once their discrepancy stops shrinking and it reaches a target fraction of the teacher's success rate, after which training proceeds with RL alone. Across Qwen2.5 models from 1.5B to 7B, RetireOPD improves ALFWorld success rate over RL baseline by 14.1% to 18.8% and WebShop accuracy by 11.8% to 19.0%, and surpasses its own skill-conditioned teacher in every setting.

</details>


### [171] [JEPA-Anything: Learning Predictive Models across Different Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20800)
*Taoyong Cui,Zhongyao Wang,Xinyue Xu,Weiyang Liu,Zhaochen Yu,Yuying Zhang,Qiang Gao,Mengyue Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JEPA-Anything 提出一种基于正交预测分解（OPF）的域无关世界建模框架，在视觉、生物、临床、控制、分子动力学、物理场和天气等七个领域展现了统一的分解式预测原则，并在动力学预测、干预预测和科学发现上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型往往是领域特定的，缺乏一个可以在截然不同的系统之间通用的世界建模学习原则。作者希望回答：是否存在一种共同的学习原理，能支撑跨越视觉、生物、物理等异构领域的预测与干预。

Method: 提出 JEPA-Anything 框架，核心是正交预测分解（Orthogonal Predictive Factorization, OPF）。它扩展联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），将潜在目标分解为互补因子，通过专用通路分别学习各因子，再在共享的预测设计内重新组合，从而实现域无关的世界建模。

Result: 在七个领域评估：在 10 个动力学任务上全面超越匹配的 JEPA 基线；在 Interventional Pong 上将单次干预预测误差降低 34.8%；在四个分子系统中取得一步与 100 步预测的最低误差；因子提名的生物干预在细胞共培养、类器官、肿瘤片段和小鼠实验中得到支持；潜轨道模态拟合出的开普勒标度指数斜率为 -1.4991（接近理论值 -1.5）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一个共同的因子化预测原则可以跨越异质世界发挥作用，将世界建模与干预预测以及有实验依据的科学发现连接起来，验证了 OPF 作为通用世界建模原则的有效性。

Abstract: World modeling enables intelligence to anticipate consequences, guide interventions, and learn from interaction. Yet predictive models remain domain-specific: can a common learning principle support world modeling across radically different systems? We introduce JEPA-Anything, a domain-agnostic framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization (OPF). Extending joint-embedding predictive architectures, OPF decomposes latent targets into complementary factors, learns them through dedicated pathways, and recombines them within a shared predictive design. We evaluate JEPA-Anything across seven domains: vision, biology, clinical trajectories, control, molecular dynamics, physical fields, and weather. Experiments span representation learning, intervention prediction, out-of-distribution generalization, and long-horizon dynamics, including 10 matched dynamics tasks, forecasting of over 1,000 clinical events, and 100-step molecular rollouts across four systems. Against matched JEPA baselines, JEPA-Anything improves reported metrics on all 10 dynamics tasks and reduces single-intervention prediction error on Interventional Pong by 34.8%. It achieves the lowest one-step and 100-step molecular errors among compared methods in all four systems. Beyond prediction, a factor-nominated biological intervention receives experimental support in cell co-cultures, patient-derived organoids, tumor fragments, and mice; latent orbital modes recover the Keplerian scaling exponent with a fitted slope of -1.4991. These results support a common factorized predictive principle across heterogeneous worlds, connecting world modeling with intervention and experimentally grounded scientific discovery. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/JEPA-Anything

</details>


### [172] [Unifying Models of Intergroup Hostility in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20808)
*Patrick Gerard,Julia Mendelsohn,Kristina Lerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于2024年美国大选期间TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X上的286万条帖子，把六种经典的群际敌意理论机制（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊、负面评价、非人化、行动导向）纳入统一实证框架，揭示敌意修辞的结构与时间组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敌对修辞会助长排斥、不公对待与政治暴力，但解释敌意的社会心理学、道德心理学和政治学理论长期各自发展，彼此缺乏对话、甚至相互矛盾，且很少在真实语料中被直接检验，导致对敌意修辞机制的理解碎片化。

Method: 收集2024年美国大选期间286万条来自TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X的帖子，在统一实证框架内对六种基础理论的机制（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊、负面评价、非人化、行动导向）进行建模，并分析其结构与时间上的组织关系。

Result: 结构上，边界建构和威胁建构是敌意修辞系统的锚点；时间上，机制呈现规律性次序：边界建构、贬损和行动导向较早出现，非人化和威胁建构较晚，替罪羊最晚出现。

Conclusion: 通过梳理这些理论框架在真实语料中的实际表现，研究弥合了社会科学各传统之间的长期分歧，并为计算社会科学超越单一标签检测、更综合地建模群际敌意修辞提供了更清晰的实证基础。

Abstract: Hostile rhetoric toward social groups can normalize exclusion and justify mistreatment, as well as contribute to rising polarization and political violence. Efforts to moderate hostile rhetoric in online speech draw on foundational theories in social and moral psychology, and political science. However, these theories were developed largely in parallel, often propose different and sometimes conflicting accounts of how hostility develops, and have rarely been tested against each other in real discourse. The result is a fragmented understanding of the rhetorical mechanisms of hostility, without a clear sense of how they appear, and relate to each other, in real-world discourse. Using 2.86 million posts from TikTok, Truth Social, and Twitter/X during the 2024 U.S. presidential election, we model the mechanisms of six foundational theories of intergroup hostility -- boundary construction, threat construction, scapegoating, negative evaluation, dehumanization, and action orientation -- within a common empirical framework to recover the broader organization of intergroup hostility rhetoric. Structurally, we find that boundary construction and threat construction anchor the system; temporally, we find that these mechanisms tend to follow a regular ordering: boundary construction, derogation, and action orientation tend to appear early; dehumanization and threat construction later; scapegoating latest. Mapping how these theoretical frameworks actually manifest in discourse bridges longstanding divisions across social science traditions and presents computational social science with a clearer empirical foundation for modeling intergroup hostility rhetoric beyond single-label detection.

</details>


### [173] [Embedding Models Measure in Peculiar Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20821)
*Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文考察词嵌入空间是否准确反映物理量（质量、距离、时间、体积）的客观测量关系，发现嵌入对物理量的建模很弱，反而主要受字符串表面相似度影响，且相似度重校准无法显著改善对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理测量拥有唯一、客观的语义等价与距离定义，是检验嵌入是否真正表征语义的理想基准。作者希望探究词嵌入能否可靠地反映这种客观物理关系，从而评估嵌入空间的语义表征质量。

Method: 选取质量、距离、时间、体积四类物理量，将嵌入空间中的语义相似度/距离与真实物理测量数值进行对齐比较；进一步分析嵌入表征与字符串表面相似度（如拼写、词形）的关联程度，并尝试对相似度进行重校准，检验对齐效果能否提升。

Result: 研究发现物理测量在嵌入空间中仅被弱建模，且呈现出相当奇特的测量模式；嵌入对物理量的表征强烈受字符串表面相似度影响，重校准相似度后与真实物理关系的对齐也未显著改善。

Conclusion: 词嵌入空间并未可靠地捕获物理量的客观语义关系，其表征很大程度上被表面字符串相似度所主导，说明现有嵌入在客观语义建模方面存在明显局限。

Abstract: Embedding spaces define notions of semantic similarity and distance. We study whether those embeddings reflect physical measurements of mass, distance, time and volume, which admit a unique, objective notion of semantic equivalence and distance. We find that physical measurement is only weakly modeled in the embedding space, and that instead quite peculiar measurement patterns can be observed. Further analysis indicates that embedding representations of physical measurements are strongly influenced by superficial string similarity, and recalibration of similarity does not substantially improve the alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [FootprintRAG: Visual Analytics for Evidence Context Refinement in RAG-based Scientific Literature Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19601)
*Xingyu Liu,Yu Dong,Qizhen Yu,Shiyu Cheng,Zhe Wang,Guan Li,Guihua Shan,Dong Tian,Christy Jie Liang,Quang Vinh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: FootprintRAG 是一个由 LLM 智能体驱动的可视分析系统，将 RAG 的证据上下文变为可检查、可修改的分析对象，帮助用户在科学文献探索中追踪并修订生成摘要所依据的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 在科学文献开放式探索中，证据上下文常经过隐蔽的检索、重排、评估和过滤步骤生成，用户无法了解证据上下文如何构建、哪些证据单元被保留或丢弃，以及是否有潜在有用证据在合成前被排除。缺乏对证据上下文的透明度与可控制性。

Method: 提出 FootprintRAG 可视分析系统：将科学文献解析为文本与图表证据单元；将初始查询扩展为并行查询变体；通过多轮迭代检索与评估证据；利用 ERS（Evidence Relevance Score，证据相关性评分）排序呈现语料级补充候选。通过协调视图把检索轨迹、证据状态修订和溯源感知的摘要生成连接为用户可操控的工作流。

Result: 通过两个案例研究、一项用户研究和与代表性 RAG 系统的工作流级对比进行评估。结果表明 FootprintRAG 能帮助用户比较检索方向、修订候选证据、恢复被忽视的证据，并将生成的摘要追溯回支撑它的证据单元。

Conclusion: FootprintRAG 通过将证据上下文显式化、可检查和可修订，提升了 RAG 科学文献探索中证据构建的透明度和用户控制力，并验证了其在检索方向比较、证据修订、证据恢复与摘要溯源方面的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly used to ground large language model (LLM) outputs in scientific literature. However, in open-ended literature exploration, the evidence context used for generation is often produced through hidden retrieval, reranking, assessment, and filtering steps. Users may receive retrieval summaries without knowing how the system constructed the evidence context, which evidence units were retained or discarded, or whether potentially useful evidence was excluded before synthesis. We present FootprintRAG, an LLM-agent-powered visual analytics system for evidence context refinement in RAG-based scientific literature exploration. The core idea is to treat the RAG evidence context as an explicit, inspectable, and revisable analytical object before generation. FootprintRAG parses scientific literature into text and figure evidence units, expands an initial query into parallel query variants, retrieves and assesses evidence across iterative rounds, and surfaces ERS-ranked supplementary candidates from the corpus-level evidence space. Through coordinated views, the system connects retrieval trajectories, evidence-state revision, and provenance-aware summary generation into a user-steerable workflow. We evaluate FootprintRAG through two case studies, a user study, and a workflow-level comparison with representative RAG systems. The results show that FootprintRAG helps users compare retrieval directions, revise candidate evidence, recover potentially overlooked evidence, and trace generated summaries back to supporting evidence units. FootprintRAG is available at https://github.com/meteorshowering/FootprintRAGVA.git.

</details>


### [175] [DELUGE: Decomposed Entropy-coded Live Unstructured Geometry Exchange for Real-time Particle Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19750)
*Hikari Yanagawa,Yuichi Hiroi,Takefumi Hiraki*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出DELUGE，一种面向粒子物理模拟（流体、烟雾、颗粒介质）的流式压缩架构，利用粒子在时间上的连贯性与速度可预测性，实现亚帧延迟的编码与解码，以支持VR/AR中多用户实时协作体验。实验表明其解码速度约为G-PCC的20倍、编码速度约为Draco的6倍，并在浏览器与Apple Vision Pro上完成了端到端实现与感知质量/协作任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理模拟对沉浸式VR/AR的视觉真实感至关重要；随着社交VR与数字孪生兴起，多用户实时共享同一模拟的需求增长。但现有点云压缩方法（G-PCC、Draco）假设静态几何结构，用于动态粒子流时编码延迟超过帧周期，无法满足实时交付要求。

Method: 提出DELUGE流式压缩架构，利用物理模拟粒子固有的时间相干性和速度可预测性，通过三种互补技术实现亚帧延迟的编码与解码。

Result: 在动态点云数据集上，解码速度约为G-PCC（TMC13）的20倍，编码速度约为Draco的6倍。在网页浏览器和Apple Vision Pro上完成端到端客户端实现，并通过被试内感知质量评估与双人协作任务研究（Vision Pro上手部追踪流体交互）验证了实时协作体验的可行性。

Conclusion: DELUGE通过挖掘粒子仿真数据的时序特性，克服了传统静态点云压缩在实时流式场景下延迟过高的瓶颈，能够支撑多用户实时协作的粒子交互体验，为社交VR与数字孪生应用提供了有效的低延迟流式方案。

Abstract: Particle-based physics simulations, including fluids, smoke, and granular media, are fundamental to visual realism in immersive VR and AR. With the growing adoption of social VR and digital twins, demand is increasing for shared experiences in which multiple users interact with the same simulation in real time. Realizing such experiences requires low-latency streaming of large-scale particle data from a server to each client, yet existing point cloud compression methods such as G-PCC (TMC13) and Draco assume static geometric structures; when applied to dynamic particle streaming, their encoding latency exceeds the frame period, failing to meet real-time delivery requirements. We propose DELUGE, a streaming compression architecture that exploits the temporal coherence and velocity predictability inherent in physics simulation particles, achieving sub-frame-latency encoding and decoding through three complementary techniques. Evaluation on dynamic point cloud datasets demonstrates that DELUGE achieves approximately $20\times$ faster decoding than G-PCC (TMC13) and $6\times$ faster encoding than Draco. We further build end-to-end client implementations for both web browsers and Apple Vision Pro, and confirm through a within-participants perceptual quality evaluation and a two-person collaborative task study on Vision Pro that the proposed method supports real-time collaborative experiences with hand-tracked fluid interaction.

</details>


### [176] [Printing the Underdetermined: Materializing Multi-solutionness in Figurative Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19782)
*Yutao Ming,Teng Xu,Youjia Wang,Yunyang Liu,Fengmin Yang,Fuqiang Zhao,Jingyi Yu,Hua Yang,Yanjun Zhou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算工作流，不再将具象绘画视为单一可恢复的3D场景，而是突出其"多解性"——一幅平面画作可对应多种3D解释；通过生成多段相机轨道视频、用3D高斯泼溅重建，并将结果实体化，使非唯一性变得可见、可比较、可讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将具象绘画当作描绘单一可恢复3D场景来处理，致力于推断深度与遮挡并收敛到唯一稳定模型；但作者认为绘画的3D解释本质上是非唯一的，这种多解性应被正视和保留，而非作为误差消除。

Method: 多解性来源有二：未观测内容（背面与遮挡体积存在多种合理补全）和已观测线索（透视、明暗、遮挡仍不足以完全约束几何）。工作流为：先用视频生成模型（无显式3D约束）从一幅绘画采样多条相机轨道多视图视频序列，帧间小幅漂移不可避免；再用3D Gaussian Splatting将每条序列重建为点式高斯场景表示，密度光晕与重影暴露未解决的自由度；最后用DreamPrinting将表示制作成实体物件。

Result: 该管线把多种兼容的3D解释作为显式输出而非残差误差，生成可被检查、比较和讨论的数字与实体形式，使绘画空间解读中的非唯一性得以物化呈现。

Conclusion: 通过将多解性视为核心特征而非缺陷，本文为具象绘画的空间解读提供了一个计算框架，支持以数字和物理两种形态对多种可能解释进行审视与讨论。

Abstract: Figurative paintings are often approached as if they depict a single recoverable 3D scene: viewers infer depth and occlusion, and reconstruction pipelines attempt to converge to one stable model. We instead foreground multi-solutionness, the non-uniqueness of 3D configurations compatible with a single painted image, and propose a workflow that keeps this non-uniqueness visible and material. Multi-solutionness arises from two sources: unobserved content, where backsides and occluded volumes admit multiple plausible completions, and observed cues, where perspective, shading, and occlusion still underconstrain geometry. When additional views are synthesized by a video generative model without explicit 3D constraints, small frame-level drifts become inevitable rather than exceptional. Our pipeline samples multiple camera-orbit multi-view video sequences from one painting, reconstructs each sequence with 3D Gaussian Splatting into a point-based Gaussian scene representation where density halos and ghosting expose unresolved degrees of freedom, and fabricates these representations as physical artifacts using DreamPrinting. By treating multiple compatible interpretations as explicit outputs rather than residual error, we provide a computational framework for spatial readings of figurative painting that can be inspected, compared, and discussed in both digital and physical form.

</details>


### [177] [An Elementary Expression for Multiple Scattering in Homogeneous Microflake Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20394)
*Jonathan Dupuy*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于微面元理论的新型漫反射类BRDF，首次在Smith遮蔽假设下以初等表达式精确涵盖所有散射阶数，同时支持解析求值与重要性采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漫反射微面元BRDF要么无法精确计入所有散射阶数，要么依赖随机（蒙特卡洛）模拟，缺乏同时具备解析求值、解析采样且精确覆盖全阶散射的构造方法，作者旨在回答此类构造是否可能。

Method: 基于微面元理论与Smith遮蔽假设推导新型漫反射类BRDF，通过解析推导得到可精确表征所有散射阶数的初等表达式，并为其设计对应的解析重要性采样策略。

Result: 该BRDF在垂直入射时表现为Lambertian反射，随着入射角趋近掠射角，反射光逐渐向镜面反射方向集中；与Lambertian及当前最先进的随机微面元BRDF渲染对比验证了这一效果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明在Smith遮蔽假设下，可以构造出既具备初等解析表达式、又支持解析重要性采样、且精确涵盖所有散射阶数的漫反射类微面元BRDF，并展示其在掠射角下的方向性反射特性。

Abstract: We derive a novel diffuse-like BRDF based on microfacet theory. Its main novelty lies in the fact that it admits an elementary expression for both evaluation and importance sampling, while accounting exactly for all scattering orders under the Smith shadowing assumption. As such, it is the first to settle the question of whether such a construction was possible. In practice, the BRDF produces Lambertian reflectance at normal incidence and progressively concentrates reflected light around the mirror direction as incidence approaches grazing. We showcase this effect by comparing rendering results against Lambertian and state-of-the-art stochastic microfacet BRDFs.

</details>


### [178] [S4R: Scaling for Rigid-Body Interpenetration Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20524)
*Zhiyang Dou,Ang Zhao,Chen Peng,Minghao Guo,Haixu Wu,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu,Junfeng Yao,Xiaohu Guo,Wenping Wang,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: S4R 是一种用于静态刚体互穿的尺度连续修复方法：先将所有刚体均匀缩小至无穿透的小尺度，再通过一系列最小范数凸接触二次规划（QP）逐步恢复全尺度，从而将一次大深度修正分解为多次浅接触子问题，在多个基准上以最低耗时实现零穿透且位移小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化组装与生成场景中频繁出现刚体互穿，必须在物理仿真等下游应用前将其移除。直接一次深度修正难度大且易引入大位移，因此需要一种稳健、高效且位移可控的静态穿透修复方法。

Method: 提出 S4R 尺度连续方法：1) 将每个刚体绕固定参考中心均匀缩小至保证无穿透的初始小尺度；2) 通过一系列针对线性化分离裕度的最小范数凸接触 QP 逐步恢复全尺度，把深度修正分解为浅接触子问题；3) 利用保守尺度事件界和冻结见证间隙预测减少精确网格查询次数；4) 最终以全尺度评估器检查并进行有界尾部细化；同时提供 GPU 实现以支持大规模场景。

Result: 在 Kubric、HY3D-Bench 和 Thingi10K 三个基准上（最高 N=5000 个刚体），S4R 达到零报告穿透，位移小且几乎与场景规模无关，并在各硬件层级中相较对比方法实现最低墙钟耗时；GPU 实现进一步扩展到大规模场景。

Conclusion: S4R 通过尺度连续策略有效解决静态刚体互穿修复问题，在零穿透、小位移和高效率之间取得良好平衡，可作为程序化与生成场景的下游预处理工具，其代码和数据已公开发布。

Abstract: Rigid-body interpenetration frequently occurs in procedurally assembled and generated scenes and must be removed before downstream applications such as physical simulation. We present S4R (Scaling for Rigid-Body Interpenetration Resolution), a scale-continuation method for static interpenetration repair. S4R first uniformly shrinks each body about a fixed reference center to a small initial scale, at which the layout is penetration-free, and then restores full scale through a sequence of minimum-norm convex contact quadratic programs (QPs) that target the linearized separation margin during continuation. Resolution thereby replaces one deep correction with a sequence of shallow-contact subproblems. A conservative scale-event bound and frozen-witness gap predictions cut the number of exact mesh queries; the continuation then ends with a full-scale evaluator check and bounded tail refinement. We evaluate S4R on Kubric, HY3D-Bench, and Thingi10K using a shared mesh-level evaluator and a unified per-scene timing protocol. In the main comparisons on all three benchmarks, up to N=5000 bodies, S4R reaches zero reported penetration with displacement that stays small and nearly independent of scene size, and at the lowest wall time within each hardware tier among the compared methods. A GPU implementation extends these results to large-scale scenes. Our code and data can be found on our project page: https://frank-zy-dou.github.io/projects/S4R/index.html.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究 emphasis temporal-difference (ETD) 学习在常数步长采样动态下的稳定性问题，提出正则化 emphatic TD (RETD) 方法，通过泄漏标量状态存储 ETD 信号并释放延迟校正，实现了对 ETD 均衡点的一阶后冲击修复，并证明了几乎必然收敛性与常数步长矩收缩性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD 虽然能稳定期望的离策略 TD 更新并改变投影几何，但这两种性质都无法决定常数步长采样动态的行为。作者构造了一个遍历双态反例，其中 ETD 均值映射收缩，但采样乘积具有正的最高 Lyapunov 指数，说明采样动态可能发散。这揭示了 ETD 在常数步长下与均值映射性质脱节的问题，需要一种能修复采样动态稳定性的方法。

Method: 作者提出正则化 emphatic TD (RETD)，这是一种归一化的一阶后冲击修复方法：保持迹与重要性比不变，将 emphatic TD 信号存储于一个泄漏标量状态中，并释放一个延迟校正。RETD 的原始均衡是 ETD 均衡的仿射平移；通过单重和双重正则化读出可精确恢复 ETD 不动点。分析采用再生循环分析分离了 Lyapunov 指数符号与 follow-on 迹的无限方差，并利用马尔可夫随机乘积界证明了谐波递减步长下的几乎必然收敛，以及条件常数步长矩收缩结果。

Result: RETD 在双态构造和 Baird 反例的一个点上具有经证明的负指数（即稳定），而正 Baird ETD 符号仍仅为数值结果。配对的 10,000 次运行实验验证了两类分离、不动点恢复、非单调稳定区域以及任务依赖性。RETD 改变了后冲击动态，但并未降低共享的 follow-on 迹方差。

Conclusion: RETD 通过后冲击修复机制，在保持 ETD 均衡点可精确恢复的前提下，改善了常数步长采样动态的稳定性，并在理论上获得了几乎必然收敛与矩收缩保证，为离策略强化学习中对 ETD 的稳定性问题的理解与补救提供了新的方法。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [180] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioPhys-Bridge 是一个面向生物物理文献的循证科学推理基准数据集，包含 500 个案例、1,517 个面向智能体的任务，覆盖六大生物领域和九大物理模型族，旨在评估语言模型在归因、忠实性、幻觉抑制和生物实验设计方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在分析跨学科科学文献时面临独特挑战：生物物理学研究中的可靠回答需要将观测数据锚定到源证据、通过定量物理模型解释，并将其与生物机制联系起来。现有基准缺乏对这类证据锚定、定量计算与机制关联相结合的多步骤推理评估。

Method: 作者构建了 BioPhys-Bridge 数据集：每个案例包含证据块、稳定的证据 ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制和下一步决策，作为问题回答（QA）与检索增强生成（RAG）的锚定目标。数据集执行严格的模式、证据完整性、定量锚定、来源许可、去重和单位归一化等质量门控，其中 81 个案例经过领域专家评审与标注。初步评测对比了 DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Qwen3.7-Max 和 GPT-4o-mini 等模型的证据 ID F1 分数。

Result: 初步评估显示 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 取得最高的证据 ID F1 分数（0.360），其后为 Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）和 GPT-4o-mini（0.294），表明现有模型在此类循证多步骤科学推理任务上仍有较大提升空间。数据集初始版本覆盖六大生物领域和九大物理模型族，其中三个稀疏模型族预留用于未来扩展。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge 为评估语言模型在复杂、多步骤科学推理中的归因、忠实性、幻觉抑制和生物实验设计能力提供了跨学科基准。未来工作将扩大数据集的规模与复杂度，并开展更全面的评估；代码与数据已在 GitHub 与 Hugging Face 上公开。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [181] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统梳理了2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇提出或更新评估资源的论文，刻画LLM评估基准在目标系统、领域、材料与评分机制上的演变，发现评估日益强调行动、交互与专业应用，且LLM评分增长不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM进展的认知主要依赖模型排行榜，但排行榜难以反映评估需求本身的变化。作者希望从基准的扩张这一视角出发，理解研究者期望LLM完成什么、以及何种表现被视为成功。

Method: 收集2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇引入或更新评估资源的论文，采用分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，分析目标系统与领域、评估材料与条件、评分机制三个维度的变化。

Result: 评估集合对行动、交互和专业应用的强调不断增长，新旧设计元素常并存。模型参与发展不均衡：LLM评分在智能体与非智能体组内均增长，而模型生成材料在近期队列中未出现同等的持续上升。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了公共研究如何将能力期望转化为具体测试与成功标准；同时引发疑问——AI同时参与出题、执行任务与评分，评估的扩张究竟是提供更独立的证据，还是有复现其参与模型偏好与盲点的风险。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [182] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张构建"基础模型操作系统"（FMOS），将易碎的、隐式运行时的智能体框架统一为一层系统抽象，如同操作系统对硬件的虚拟化，以解决复合智能体系统的可移植性与治理难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 应用已从单一基础模型转向复合智能体系统，但技术栈仍然碎片化：尽管 MCP、A2A 等协议降低了工具/智能体连接的门槛，各框架仍各自内嵌隐式运行时（状态、记忆、预算、护栏），导致行为不可移植、治理脆弱。这类似于操作系统诞生前每个程序都要重新实现基础服务的时代。

Method: 提出 FMOS 系统层：对外虚拟化基础模型交互（类比虚拟机抽象硬件），为应用提供"专用、可信、能力近乎无限"的 FM 实例幻象；对内负责跨记忆分层编排知识、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行。同时借鉴人脑"快直觉 vs 慢深思"的双系统机制，学习何时干预、何时让推理直接进行，并根据运行经验持续自适应策略。

Result: 本文为立场性论文（position paper），提出 FMOS 作为统一抽象层的构想与设计原则，而非给出具体实验数据或量化结果。

Conclusion: 领域需要向 FMOS 演进，以系统层虚拟化来收敛碎片化的智能体栈，使复合智能体应用具备可移植、可治理、可持续自适应的基础设施。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [183] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TranSGrid 测试平台，将演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理统一到一个任务中，用以更全面地衡量系统泛化能力。实验表明，Transformer 模型在 TranSGrid 上表现远差于普通留出测试集，且该差距出现在训练长度范围内，说明仅用生产力(组合泛化)不足以评估系统泛化；若单独消除归纳或溯因需求，模型表现回升至普通测试集水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统泛化研究依赖于若干简化（近似线性的动作组合、基于生产力的测试、动作显式的目标），从而遗漏了系统泛化的某些核心方面。作者希望厘清这些简化究竟忽略了什么，并以推理为核心视角，统一考察演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理，从而更全面地度量系统泛化。

Method: 作者从推理出发，设计并引入 TranSGrid 测试平台，将演绎、归纳、溯因推理整合到同一个统一任务中。随后用 7 个 Transformer 模型在 4,800 个 TranSGrid 实例上进行实验，并将其与留出测试集（held-out test set）表现进行对比。此外，作者还将原先被省略的两类简化——近似线性动作组合（降低归纳需求）与动作显式目标（降低溯因需求）——分别重新引入 TranSGrid，以检验这些简化对模型表现的影响。

Result: 所有 7 个 Transformer 在 TranSGrid 上的表现都远差于留出测试集：最大模型在测试集上解出 79.6%，但在 TranSGrid 上仅 55.3%，在最难子集上仅 15.8%。这一差距存在于训练长度范围内，表明单纯的生产力不足以评估系统泛化。重新引入两类简化中的任一者后，模型解决率均回升至接近测试集水平，说明任一简化单独就足以把 TranSGrid 降级为普通留出测试集。

Conclusion: 现有任务通常降低了归纳和/或溯因需求，而全面度量系统泛化需要一个同时包含演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理的任务；TranSGrid 正是这样一个更严格、更完整的基准。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [184] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是首个针对四大对话平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek）的智能体网络搜索端到端生命周期的研究，结合真实用户交互与 API 对照实验，揭示了搜索决策、查询策略、领域偏好及引用可信度等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式 LLM 智能体越来越依赖网络搜索，但其端到端生命周期—从是否调用搜索、如何构造查询、接收何种搜索结果到如何生成有依据的回答—尚缺乏系统理解，因此需要对其进行全面研究。

Method: 采用 in-vivo（真实用户交互）与 in-vitro（通过 API 使用同一平台模型进行受控实验）相结合的方式，对 ChatGPT、Claude、Grok 和 DeepSeek 四大平台的网络搜索进行对比研究，考察调用决策质量、查询构造策略、搜索结果领域偏好以及回应生成时的引用选择。

Result: 研究发现：各平台及模型之间的网络搜索调用决策差异显著，更频繁地调用搜索并不一定带来更好的回答质量；对话智能体采用多样且复杂的查询策略；各平台专属搜索引擎倾向于返回其偏好的领域结果；虽然大多数回答基于搜索结果，但部分声明依赖未引用的搜索结果，存在归因与可靠性隐患。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了对话式检索中搜索调用、查询策略、领域偏好与引用实践等方面的系统性问题，对未来 AI 智能体及面向对话检索优化的网络搜索工具设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [185] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoTuning 评估框架，将同一 15 维加速器设计空间以"具名架构旋钮"和"匿名 [0,1] 变量"两种方式交给同一智能体，通过两者表现的差距来测量智能体是否真正"理解"了硬件，而非盲目搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究只报告智能体"改进了设计"，却无法区分它是在"推理机器"还是在"熟练搜索无意义旋钮"；只有前者能迁移到下一架构。现有评测只变化智能体、固定问题框架，故无法区分二者。作者反其道而行之，固定智能体、改变问题的"意义性"。

Method: AutoTuring 将同一智能体、同一 15 维加速器空间交办两次：一次是以具名架构旋钮（配有模拟器计数器）呈现，一次是以 [0,1] 上的匿名变量呈现；评测器、合法空间和可达最优解完全一致，唯一变化是问题是否"有意义"。二者差距即为测量。在九核 FP16 GEMM 基准上对比架构智能体与盲智能体，并引入 critic 循环测试搜索式批判与架构知识的替代/互补关系。

Result: 在九核 FP16 GEMM 任务上，架构智能体平均比建模的 H200 快 5.4%、比盲智能体快 12.3%，且少用 70.1% 的模拟器调用。但这一优势并不唯一：critic 循环为盲智能体追回了大部分差距，却对架构智能体无益，表明架构知识与结构化批判互为替代而非互补。

Conclusion: 作者报告的是初步发现（每条件 5-6 次运行、单一建模加速器），强调贡献在于"对比方法本身"而非具体加速器；并指出架构知识与结构化批判呈替代关系。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [186] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体框架 MAGS，以 Dafny 为可验证中间表示，在冻结的人工审计 API 与安全需求下，自动生成带形式化安全保证的可执行程序，并在 220 个样例上实现 100% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体大规模生成复杂程序，人工审查愈发困难，安全与安全失败风险上升。模糊测试、静态分析和 LLM-as-a-Verifier 虽能发现许多失败，但难以覆盖所有边界情况；形式化验证可提供机器可检查的保证，但传统上需要大量人工撰写规格与证明工作。

Method: 提出统一的多智能体框架 MAGS：将人工审计过的 API 与安全需求形式化并冻结；使用 Dafny 作为可验证感知的中间表示，机械检查安全属性；将生成代码翻译为 Dafny；利用验证器反馈修复违规；并将通过验证的程序编译回可执行代码。

Result: 在 100 个 CUDA 内核、100 个终端脚本和 20 个机械臂任务上，共 220 个样例全部达到 100% 成功率，生成的程序均具备针对冻结规格的非平凡安全保证。独立的安全与功能评估显示三个领域均表现强劲，但也暴露出当自动形式化语义未能完全捕获目标行为时的失败。

Conclusion: MAGS 证明以 Dafny 为中间表示、结合冻结的审计规格与验证器反馈修复的多智能体方法，能在大规模程序生成中可靠地提供形式化安全保证，实现 100% 的成功率；同时指出自动形式化语义覆盖不足是当前的主要失败来源。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [187] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一项测量与基准研究，提出工具幻觉五分类法（H1-H5）和"Resolution Rung"这一训练无关、封闭世界的解析器作为参照点，论证幻觉防御必须先于任何因果门控；并在十个托管模型、两种调用界面下测得322个真实幻觉，进而扩展到模型上下文协议（MCP），发现多服务器合并会结构性产生碰撞与遮蔽，测得154个幻觉（含此前单一注册表中干净的 frontier 模型）。作者发布了带版本的 Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强LLM智能体的防御（工具选择与工具门控）都预设发出的调用指向真实存在的工具，因此无法处理"工具幻觉"——调用不存在的工具或传递schema未声明的参数。这是一种结构性盲区：幻觉调用在构造上就不是任何门控做出的决策，故任何门控都无法拒绝它。本文旨在测量并刻画这一被忽视的失败模式。

Method: 本文以测量和基准研究为主要方法：提出工具幻觉五分类法（H1-H5），引入"Resolution Rung"这一训练无关、封闭世界解析器（注册表成员资格+签名检查）作为参照点；证明幻觉防御必须在因果门控之前，并刻画唯一不可约残差（借用参数在schema上与合法调用不可区分）。在十个托管模型、两种调用表面（受限与不受限的raw-JSON）上测量幻觉；再扩展到MCP，将多个服务器合并为一个命名空间，提出第二分类法（M1-M5），并在实时MCP表面测量幻觉。

Result: 测得322个真实幻觉；虚构工具调用集中于不受限的raw-JSON表面（34 vs. 3），且模型规模无帮助（675B模型与7-8B模型相当）。在MCP多服务器合并场景中，单个注册表无法表达由此产生的幻觉表面（碰撞与遮蔽是合并的结构性结果），在实时MCP表面测得154个幻觉，包括在单一注册表表面上原本干净的frontier模型。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉是现有工具选择与工具安全方法都无法解决的结构性失败模式；幻觉防御必须位于任何因果门控之前，且存在一个不可约残差（借用参数不可区分）。多工具服务器合并（如MCP）会引入注册表无法表达的新幻觉表面。作者发布带版本的Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB)，使任何解析器可在不同提交间进行比较。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [188] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从表征视角审视"目标"概念，指出目标如同语言一样具有"形式—意义"（句法—语义）的区分，并提出不同目标表征构成一个可比较的设计空间，其关键维度包括表达力、设计选择与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究常把目标视为固定的、约束行为的认知状态，却很少关注目标表征本身的约束与性质。作者旨在借鉴语言学与逻辑学中句法—语义接口的思路，凸显"目标作为表征及其内容"这一平行关系，并由此提出关于目标表征表达力、设计与效率的基础性问题。

Method: 本文是一篇综合性（综述/理论）论文：不是进行新的实验，而是综合现有工作，将不同目标表征的性质加以刻画，并把这些性质归纳为一个更广泛的设计空间的各个节点；并借助"形式与意义"的区分来系统分析目标表征。

Result: 论文将目标表征的三个核心维度（或轴）归纳为：表达力（某一目标语言是否能充分刻画所关注的行为）、设计选择（不同目标表征的构造方式）与效率；并指出不同目标表征可能刻画相同行为，从而存在等价的表征选择。

Conclusion: 区分目标的"形式"与"意义"有助于揭示我们对目标所持有的隐含假设，为研究高阶认知与动机之间的交互提供新视角，并为不同目标概念厘清可变的维度（轴）。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [189] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖图框架将组合推理形式化为三个复杂度递增的层级，并用数据结构任务进行实证，发现"分解技能训练难以迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更容易迁移回分解任务"的不对称性，并从理论上加以解释，同时考察了长度外推、分布偏移、未知技能迁移和现实工具调用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题的解决高度依赖组合推理——由于训练数据必然有限，模型必须学会以新方式组合已学技能来泛化。尽管强化学习等后训练方法显著提升了语言模型的推理能力，但其对组合推理的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 提出依赖图（dependency-graph）框架，将组合推理形式化为三个复杂度递增的层级；以数据结构任务为实例，利用其确定性奖励和清晰的组合结构进行实证研究；针对观察到的分解-组合不对称性给出理论解释；此外还评估了长度外推、结构分布偏移、涉及未知技能的迁移，并在真实工具调用基准上开展试点研究。

Result: 发现一致的"分解到组合"不对称性：分解技能训练不能可靠地迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练则更容易迁移回分解任务；该不对称性在长度外推、结构分布偏移和未知技能迁移等设置下得到进一步验证；工具调用基准的试点研究初步表明该不对称性可扩展到实际场景。

Conclusion: 组合任务训练与分解技能训练之间的迁移存在方向性不对称，这一发现对如何设计训练策略以提升语言模型的组合推理能力具有指导意义，并提示单纯依赖分解技能训练不足以应对现实世界中需要技能新组合的泛化问题。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills. Finally, we present a pilot study on real-world tool-calling benchmarks, showing preliminary evidence that the decomposed-to-composed asymmetry can extend to practical settings.

</details>


### [190] [Safety Beyond the Interface: Detecting Harm via Latent States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19472)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu,Omkar Neogi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用 LLM 内部激活训练轻量级 MLP 探针来检测有害提示，其效果可与大 1000 倍的护栏模型匹敌，同时大幅降低延迟与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统日益依赖大语言模型，但围绕这些模型的安全基础设施带来延迟和计算开销，限制了其在资源受限、时间关键场景中的可用性；现有外部护栏模型无法洞察模型内部机制，形成了根本性的保证缺口。因此提出核心问题：模型是否已经"知道"内容是有害的？

Method: 从 LLaMA-3.1-8B 中提取激活，训练轻量级 MLP 分类器探针（参数量 12.6M）来检测有害提示；在 WildJailbreak、Beavertails 和 AEGIS 2.0 三个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 探针在三个数据集上分别取得 F1 分数 99%、83% 和 84%，性能与大 1000 倍的护栏模型相当，同时显著降低了延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: 模型内部激活已包含足够的有害性信息，轻量探针可提供高效、低成本的安全检测，有望弥合外部护栏因不了解内部机制而产生的保证缺口，适用于资源受限与时间关键的安全部署。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) yet the safety infrastructure surrounding these models introduces latency and compute overhead. This limits utility in resource-constrained, time-critical deployments. Existing external guardrail models remain blind to the model's internal workings, creating a fundamental assurance gap. We ask: does the model already know when the content is harmful? We extract activations from LLaMA-3.1-8B and train lightweight MLP classifier probes (12.6M parameters) to detect harmful prompts. Evaluated on WildJailbreak, Beavertails, and AEGIS 2.0, our probes achieve F1 scores of 99%, 83%, and 84%, respectively competitive with 1000x larger guard models while cutting latency and compute costs.

</details>


### [191] [QVAC Genesis III: A Large-Scale, High-Quality Open Synthetic STEM Corpus for Efficient Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19513)
*Davide Vitabile,N. Ranjan,Akshay Nambiar,Kamal K. Gupta,Amril Nazir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 QVAC Genesis III，一个 191.43B token、面向 STEM 的 19 领域多层级合成语料库，通过"弱学生模型信号驱动的教师蒸馏+选项级对比推理"双重生成策略构建，并用 LLM-as-a-parser 协议评测；1.7B 模型从头训练消融实验显示其在 ARC、GPQA Diamond、MMLU STEM 上全面超越 Cosmopedia-v2 与 Cosmo-1B，ARC-E/ARC-C 最高提升 28.57%/21.35%，有效答案率达 99.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向边缘 AI 与端侧部署的小模型对 token 预算极为受限，而高质量预训练数据是核心瓶颈；主流机构用私有语料训练更大模型，开源生态却缺乏 STEM 专向、能以高"单 token 学习价值"高效服务小模型的合成数据集。

Method: 构建 QVAC Genesis III 合成语料：覆盖 19 个 STEM 领域、多难度层级与多种教学风格，总量 191.43B token。采用双重生成策略——①以弱边缘规模学生模型为信号进行定向教师蒸馏：将学生失败案例转化为纠错式解释，将学生成功案例扩展为覆盖所有选项的对比式选项级推理；②引入 LLM-as-a-parser 评测协议，从自由形式输出中抽取最终答案并同时追踪准确率与答案有效性。通过 1.7B 参数模型的受控从头训练消融实验验证数据有效性。

Result: 在 ARC、GPQA Diamond、MMLU STEM 基准上，用 QVAC Genesis III 训练的模型均一致优于开源合成语料 Cosmopedia-v2 训练模型及公开的 Cosmo-1B 模型：ARC-E 提升最高 +28.57%，ARC-C 最高 +21.35%，有效答案率最高达 99.45%。

Conclusion: QVAC Genesis III 为开源生态提供了一个高效、STEM 专向的大规模合成预训练语料，证实在 token 预算受限的边缘场景下，通过"弱学生信号引导的蒸馏与对比式选项级推理"生成的数据能显著提升小模型的 STEM 能力与答案有效性，弥补了公开 STEM 合成数据集的缺口。

Abstract: High-quality pre-training data is a critical bottleneck for educational and STEM-specific language models targeting edge AI and on-device deployment where token budgets are tightly constrained. While major organizations train ever-larger models on private corpora, the open ecosystem lacks STEM-focused synthetic datasets that deliver high per-token learning value efficiently for small models. To address this gap, we introduce QVAC Genesis III, a 191.43B-token, STEM-focused multi-domain synthetic corpus covering 19 domains across several difficulty levels and different educational styles. QVAC Genesis III is built via a dual generation strategy that performs targeted teacher distillation using a weak edge-scale student model as signal: the student's failures are converted into corrective explanations, while its successes are expanded into contrastive option-level reasoning over all answer choices. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-parser evaluation protocol that extracts final answers from free-form outputs and tracks both accuracy and answer validity. To validate the effectiveness of our QVAC Genesis III data, we conduct controlled from-scratch ablations with 1.7B-parameter models, showing that models trained with QVAC Genesis III consistently outperform both models trained with the open-source synthetic corpus Cosmopedia-v2 and the publicly released Cosmo-1B model across ARC, GPQA Diamond, and MMLU STEM benchmarks, achieving up to +28.57% on ARC-E and +21.35% on ARC-C, while reaching a Valid Answer Rate of up to 99.45%.

</details>


### [192] [LLM-as-an-Improver: Turning Verification into Better Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19515)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM-as-an-Improver 范式和 Verify--Repair--Reselect (VRR) 方法，将验证反馈不仅用于排序选择，还用于生成并重新选择更优候选解，从而超越固定候选池的验证选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于验证器的选择方法仅把验证当作排序步骤，评估完固定候选池后便丢弃反馈，未充分利用验证信息去改进候选集本身。作者希望探究验证是否也能提升候选集合的质量。

Method: 提出 VRR 方法：保留初始优胜者，并有条件地生成三类互补候选——优胜者与亚军的修复版本，以及基于新思路的解；仅利用推理时信息过滤无效和重复候选；最后在原评估准则下重选最终答案。

Result: 在多种模型及代码生成与推理基准上，VRR 在众多设定下优于固定候选池的验证选择，且即使初始候选池全部错误，VRR 仍可恢复出正确解。

Conclusion: 验证反馈不仅能从现有解中选择，还能在初始池之外构造更强的候选，展现了 LLM 作为改善者（improver）的更广泛作用。

Abstract: Verifier-based selection improves LLM performance by generating multiple candidate solutions and using a verifier to select the most promising one. However, existing methods typically treat verification only as a ranking step and discard its feedback once a fixed candidate pool has been evaluated. In this paper, we ask whether verification can also improve the candidate set itself. To this end, we introduce LLM-as-an-Improver and propose Verify--Repair--Reselect (VRR), which uses verification feedback to generate and reselect improved candidates. VRR retains the initial winner while conditionally generating three complementary alternatives: repaired versions of the winner and runner-up, and a solution based on a new approach. It filters invalid and duplicate candidates using only inference-time information and then reselects the final answer under the original evaluation criteria. Across diverse models and code-generation and reasoning benchmarks, VRR improves over fixed-pool verifier-based selection in many settings and can recover correct solutions even when all candidates in the initial pool are incorrect. These results highlight a broader role for LLMs as improvers: verification feedback can not only select among existing solutions but also construct stronger candidates beyond the initial pool.

</details>


### [193] [An Architecture for Long-Horizon Agents: Levels, Ticks and Cascaded Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19519)
*Erik Nijkamp,Anurag Koul,Egor Pakhomov,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向长期任务（跨天/周）的语言模型智能体的分层架构：按时间尺度分层、每层维护有界文件摘要、以时钟tick为自主行动单位、失败后升级到更强模型的级联智能。作者用一场为期十天的实验验证了该智能体能够跨越上下文重置与会话边界保持任务主线、早期写入的知识改变后续行为（无需改变权重），并讨论了持续学习组件应嵌入的环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体日益被要求执行持续数天甚至数周的工作（如运维修复、研究项目），这类任务超出任何上下文窗口、任何进程以及人类能够关注的任何时间间隔。作者认为，长期智能体必须先具备"不遗忘地持续运行"的能力，才能继而实现"持续学习"，且这一能力位于模型外部的"挽具（harness）"而非模型内部。

Method: 从长期任务设定中归纳出七个瓶颈，并提出一个三部分的层次化架构加以解决：(i) 按时间尺度索引的层级，每一层维护一个有界文件以摘要其下层内容；(ii) 以"时钟tick"作为自主行动的基本单位；(iii) 级联智能，即只有在审查失败后，才将工作升级到能力更强的模型。此外，通过一场为期十天、人类每天仅参与一次的实战活动（复现一项已发表的强化学习结果）对该架构进行验证。

Result: 在为期十天的活动中，基于该架构的智能体成功复现了已发表的强化学习结果。实验表明：(1) 智能体在活动的每次上下文重置与会话边界处都保持了任务主线；(2) 早期写入的操作性知识在后续改变了行为，而模型权重未发生任何变化；(3) 明确了学习组件应进入这一系统的位置（即挽具已在运行的检查环节）。

Conclusion: 这些智能体的持续学习需要一个比任何上下文和进程都更长寿的"基板（substrate）"，而学习器应嵌入到挽具已运行的检查（checks）环节之中。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out work spanning days or weeks, such as an operations remediation or a research programme. Such a task outlives any context window, any process and any interval at which a person can attend. In this paper, we argue that a long-horizon agent must run continually without forgetting before it can learn continually. This ability lies in the harness around the model rather than in the model itself. We derive seven bottlenecks from the long-horizon setting and answer them with a hierarchical architecture of three parts: (i) levels indexed by time scale, each keeping a bounded file summarising the level below; (ii) a clocked tick as the unit of autonomous action; and (iii) cascaded intelligence, where work is escalated to a more capable model only after failing review. We report on a ten-day campaign in which an agent built on this architecture reproduced a published reinforcement-learning result with a human attending once a day, and show (1) the agent kept the thread across every context reset and session boundary of the campaign, (2) operating knowledge written early changed later behaviour with no change to model weights, and (3) where learned components would enter such a system. Overall, our experience suggests continual learning for these agents needs a substrate outliving every context and process, and the checks the harness already runs are where a learner belongs.

</details>


### [194] [EconSkills: Studying Skill Transfer and Retrieval for Web Agents on Live Economic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19523)
*Yinzhu Quan,Zefang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EconSkills，一个将已验证的 EconWebArena 轨迹蒸馏为可参数化标准操作程序的技能库与评估框架，证明可复用的经济网络程序能跨任务实例迁移，并给出覆盖感知选择与上下文传递的设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 智能体经常重复访问相同站点，但大多数评估会丢弃此前成功交互中学到的程序，浪费了可复用知识；需要一种方式把已验证轨迹固化为可复用的经济数据检索程序，并系统评估其跨任务迁移与库内检索价值。

Method: 构建 EconSkills 技能库与评估框架：从 EconWebArena 已验证轨迹中蒸馏出参数化标准操作程序；每条技能记录其适用范围、导航程序、站点专属指导、验证检查与恢复步骤，并把源实例中的具体值替换为占位符。实验分离两个问题：已知相关程序能否迁移到留出任务（受控迁移），以及智能体从技能库中检索选择时能否保留该收益（库规模下的检索）。用配对成功所需的步骤数、成功率对比无技能基线与原始轨迹回放，并按覆盖分层分析结果，同时用浏览器轨迹识别程序化指导何时缩短门户专属导航以及何时仍需语义验证。

Result: 受控迁移中，匹配技能相对无技能提示提升了成功率，并在配对成功上需要更少步骤；抽象化程序显著优于直接回放原始轨迹。在库规模上，检索总体与无技能基线相当，但在被直接覆盖的任务上表现最佳；覆盖分层结果显示，在未覆盖任务上的近似匹配抵消了这些增益。浏览器轨迹进一步表明程序化指导可缩短门户专属导航，而语义验证仍不可或缺。

Conclusion: 可复用的经济网络程序能够跨任务实例迁移，为覆盖感知的选择与上下文传递提供了具体的设计目标，证明将成功交互固化为参数化程序并配合检索是提升 Web 智能体效率的有效路径。

Abstract: Web agents often revisit the same sites, yet most evaluations discard the procedures learned in earlier successful interactions. We introduce EconSkills, a skill library and evaluation framework that distills verified EconWebArena trajectories into parameterized standard operating procedures for retrieving live economic data. Each skill records its scope, navigation procedure, site-specific guidance, verification checks, and recovery steps while replacing source-instance values with placeholders. EconSkills separates two questions: whether a known relevant procedure transfers to a held-out task, and whether an agent can retain that benefit when selecting from a library. In controlled transfer, matched skills improve success over no-skill prompting and require fewer steps on paired successes, while abstraction is substantially more effective than replaying raw trajectories. At library scale, retrieval is competitive with the no-skill baseline overall and performs best on directly covered tasks; coverage-stratified outcomes show that approximate matches on uncovered tasks offset these gains. Browser trajectories further identify when procedural guidance shortens portal-specific navigation and when semantic verification remains necessary. These results establish that reusable economic web procedures can transfer across task instances and provide a concrete design target for coverage-aware selection and context delivery.

</details>


### [195] [A Unified Evaluation Framework for Trustworthy Large Language Models, Agentic AI, and Multimodal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19524)
*Shaina Raza,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Imran Liaquat,Kathryn Hume*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的信任度评估框架，将输出级、轨迹级和跨模态评估通过八个可信维度（能力、鲁棒性、安全性、公平性、透明性、治理、监督、效率）连接起来，并附加不确定性估计、可追溯证据与元评估层，以防止聚合分数掩盖关键失败，最终连接技术评估与治理/监管需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖基准分数不足以评估现代AI系统（LLM、智能体系统、多模态模型）的可信度；不同类型的系统需要不同形式的评估，但评估证据必须对开发和监督保持可解释。

Method: 提出统一框架：将输出级、轨迹级、跨模态评估映射到八个信任维度；将原生指标映射到通用性能档位，并配以不确定性估计和可追溯证据；增设元评估层检验评估本身的效度、信度与可复现性；生成多维画像；设置安全关键性覆盖机制以防止聚合分数掩盖严重失败；将结果映射到治理框架、国际标准与欧盟监管要求。

Result: 该框架为评估系统性能和支撑证据的可信度提供结构化基础，但跨部署情境的实证验证仍是必要的后续步骤。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够连接不同类型AI系统的评估并映射到治理与监管需求，弥合技术评估与监督之间的鸿沟；未来需开展跨部署场景的实证验证以完善框架。

Abstract: Benchmark scores alone provide an incomplete basis for assessing the trustworthiness of modern artificial intelligence systems. Large language models (LLMs), agentic systems, and multimodal models (MLLMs) require different forms of assessment, yet their evaluation evidence must remain interpretable for development and oversight. We propose a unified framework that connects output-level, trajectory-level, and cross-modal assessment through eight trustworthiness dimensions: capability, robustness, safety, fairness, transparency, governance, oversight, and efficiency. The framework preserves system-specific metrics while mapping native measurements to common performance bands, accompanied by uncertainty estimates and traceable evidence. A meta-evaluation layer examines the validity, reliability, and reproducibility of the evaluation itself. Multidimensional profiles expose strengths and weaknesses, while safety-critical overrides prevent aggregate scores from masking critical failures. Mappings to governance frameworks, international standards, and European Union regulatory requirements connect technical assessment with oversight needs. The framework provides a structured basis for assessing both system performance and the credibility of the evidence supporting it, with empirical validation across deployment contexts remaining an essential next step.

</details>


### [196] [Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19526)
*Xinghong Fu,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Yutaro Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIFT 提出一种样本高效的自改进框架，通过 LLM 作为裁判的成对比较信号（结合正则化 Bradley-Terry 模型）在轻量解耦树搜索中指导候选补丁探索，仅对最有希望的节点进行昂贵的下游任务评估，从而在严格预算下显著提升编码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可以通过递归修改自身实现形成自改进循环，但现有方法成本高、计算密集，主要瓶颈在于评估候选自修改时需要重跑基准测试任务子集。本文旨在在严格预算约束下实现高效的自改进。

Method: 提出 SIFT 框架：(1) 用 LLM-as-a-judge 对候选补丁做成对比较；(2) 用正则化 Bradley-Terry 模型汇总胜负记录得到强度分数；(3) 强度分数驱动轻量解耦树搜索中的基于排名的父节点采样；(4) 将昂贵的下游任务评估仅保留给最有希望的节点。

Result: 在完整 Polyglot 基准测试上，SIFT 超过了现有基于树搜索的自进化框架，且在 CPU 小时数、墙钟时间和 API 成本等资源消耗方面显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过用快速裁判信号替代/减少昂贵的下游评估，SIFT 实现了低成本、样本高效的自改进，为编码智能体的递归自我提升提供了可行的预算受限方案。

Abstract: Coding agents can recursively modify their own implementations, forming a loop of self-improvement. While prior work shows this can boost performance on coding benchmarks, existing approaches are costly and compute-intensive. We introduce a simple, sample-efficient self-improvement framework that significantly improves coding performance under strict budget constraints. We identify evaluation of candidate self-modifications as the main runtime bottleneck since prior approaches estimate their effectiveness by re-running a subset of benchmark tasks with the modified agent, which is time-consuming. We introduce Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search (SIFT), which augments these downstream task evaluations with an LLM-as-a-judge signal that performs pairwise comparisons between candidate patches, where the win-loss record is aggregated with a regularized Bradley-Terry model, and the resulting strength scores drive rank-based parent sampling inside a lightweight disaggregated tree search. Expensive downstream task evaluations are reserved only for the most promising nodes. Using a fully disaggregated tree search pipeline, the judge scores provide intermediate signal to guide exploration on promising candidate patches without being bottlenecked by slow evaluation runs. SIFT outperforms existing tree-search based self-evolution frameworks on the full Polyglot benchmark with significantly lower resource requirements in terms of CPU hours, wall clock time, and API cost.

</details>


### [197] [When Hiring Becomes Agent-Mediated: Evaluating Access and Recurrence in Two-Agent Résumé Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19530)
*Jian Gao,Hang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究双边智能体招聘筛选：雇主方与求职方智能体分别代表双方交换证据并更新判断，与传统一次性简历评分对比。在600对简历-职位上，双边程序进入率更高，且不是简单放松标准，而是双向改变决策，且其筛选结果无法用任一次性阈值复现；两智能体独有选择的复现稳定性更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘本质是双向的：雇主评估匹配度，求职者主动陈述并辩护自身资质。但作为第一道关卡的简历筛选，通常被自动化成对简历-职位对的一次性静态判断，忽视了候选人的辩护与交互能力，无法充分体现双向过程。

Method: 构建600对简历-职位样本，使用GPT-5.5与Claude Opus 4.7两个模型，对比"一次性评分"与"两智能体（雇主方+求职方）交换证据并更新判断后再决定晋级"两种程序。在共同的191对边界池上做三次重复运行，并对筛选出的案例在全新运行中复测其可复现性，另设一次性跟进作为对照。

Result: 两智能体筛选晋级更多申请（GPT-5.5：33.3%→39.3%；Opus 4.7：34.0%→35.5%）。在191对边界池的三次运行中，通过率分别从4.5%升至26.2%、从6.5%升至16.1%。这不是单方面放宽：两智能体会拒绝一次性会晋级的申请，决策双向改变。在相近通过量下，两种程序选出的申请不同，且任何一次性阈值都无法稳定复现两智能体筛选所选出的申请。在复现性上，两智能体独有的选择在新运行中重现率低于双方共同选择（GPT-5.5下明显，Opus 4.7下确定性较弱），而一次性跟进则无类似下降。

Conclusion: 当招聘两端都走向智能体中介化时，筛选"程序"本身（而非仅底层模型）决定了谁能进入人工审核，以及这种入选机会能否稳定重现。仅优化模型而忽视交互式、双边化的筛选流程设计，可能无法实现公平且可靠的招聘筛选。

Abstract: Hiring is bilateral: employers assess fit, while candidates present and defend evidence of their qualifications. Yet résumé screening, the first gate, is commonly automated as a static, one-call judgment over a résumé-job pair. We study a two-agent alternative in which employer-side and candidate-side agents represent these roles, exchange evidence, and update their judgments before deciding who advances. We compare procedures on 600 constructed résumé-job pairs using GPT-5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7. Two-agent screening advances more applications (33.3% to 39.3% for GPT-5.5; 34.0% to 35.5% for Opus 4.7). Across three runs on the common 191-pair borderline pool, pass-instance rates rise from 4.5% to 26.2% and from 6.5% to 16.1%, respectively. This is not a uniform relaxation: two-agent screening rejects applications one-call advances, changing decisions in both directions. At similar pass volumes, the procedures advance different applications, and no one-call threshold recovers applications consistently selected by two-agent screening. Among discovery-selected cases re-executed in fresh runs, two-agent-only selections recur less often than shared selections, clearly under GPT-5.5 and less certainly under Opus 4.7, while a separate one-call follow-up shows no comparable decline. As hiring becomes agent-mediated on both sides, the screening procedure, not only the model behind it, shapes who reaches human review and how reliably that access recurs.

</details>


### [198] [Agentic AI Networking for Heterogeneous Unmanned Aerial Systems in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19538)
*Nguyen Duc Minh Quang,Chang Liu,Shuangyang Li,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"层次化混合 LLM-MARL"双环架构，用于低空无线网络（LAWNs）中异构无人系统的协调共存：外环用 LLM 解析服务需求与运营意图并动态重构目标与资源优先级，内环执行参数化条件下的去中心化 MARL 策略，无需重训即可适应动态变化的运营条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络中异构无人系统的共存导致移动性、连接性与共享网络资源之间强耦合，且异构服务提出差异化、时变的需求。这种交互构成动态非合作博弈，运营条件与协调目标随时间演化。传统优化与学习控制器依赖预定义目标，难以自主适应不断变化的服务需求与资源优先级，因此需要一种能动态重构目标的自适应控制框架。

Method: 提出层次化混合 LLM-MARL 架构，组织为双环结构：(1) 外环（适应环）采用 LLM 辅助的博弈编排，解释服务需求和运营者意图，并重构目标与资源优先级；(2) 内环在所配置的博弈下执行去中心化、参数条件化的 MARL 策略。通过物流监控案例研究验证该框架在异构服务间协调共存的能力。

Result: 物流监控案例表明，该框架能促进异构服务间的协调共存，并在无需重训底层 MARL 策略的情况下适应不断演化的运营条件。

Conclusion: 所提框架为低空无线网络提供了一种自适应、无需重训的协调机制，使异构无人系统能在动态变化的服务需求与资源优先级下共存。未来需进一步研究可扩展、可信且自适应的智能体式（agentic）低空无线网络的关键挑战与研究方向。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) are emerging as a key infrastructure for heterogeneous unmanned aerial systems that support concurrent services within a shared three-dimensional airspace. Their coexistence creates strong coupling among mobility, connectivity, and shared network resources, while heterogeneous services impose distinct and time-varying requirements. These interactions naturally form a dynamic non-cooperative game in which both operating conditions and coordination objectives evolve over time. Conventional optimization and learning-based controllers typically rely on predefined objectives, limiting their ability to adapt autonomously to changing service requirements and resource priorities. To address this challenge, we propose a hierarchical hybrid large language model (LLM)- multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) architecture organized as a dual-loop structure. Specifically, an outer adaptation loop employs LLM-assisted game orchestration to interpret service requirements and operator intent, and reconfigure objectives and resource priorities, while an inner loop executes decentralized, parameter-conditioned MARL policies under the configured game. A logistics-monitoring case study illustrates how the proposed framework facilitates coordinated coexistence among heterogeneous services, adapting to evolving operating conditions without retraining the underlying MARL policies. Finally, we discuss key challenges and research directions toward scalable, trustworthy, and adaptive agentic LAWNs.

</details>


### [199] [Continual Enterprise World Model Discovery in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19551)
*Shambhavi Mishra,David Vazquez,Perouz Taslakian,Marco Pedersoli,Jose Dolz,Issam H. Laradji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持续企业世界模型发现：智能体在未知业务规则的企业系统（ServiceNow）中通过交互观察学习规则并构建可随规则变化而修订的世界模型，并在新基准 EnterpriseWorldShift 上验证其预测能力优于逐次查询的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统中，字段更新可能触发其他字段变更、创建记录或启动审批，这些效果源于各组织自行编写、随时间修订的业务规则，而非平台内置。由于智能体无法预知这些规则，也就无法预测自身行为的结果，因此需要一种能自动发现并持续维护这些业务规则表示的世界模型。

Method: 提出 Continual Discovery Agent（CDA），它从无规则知识开始，通过与记录交互并观察结果来发现业务规则，构建世界模型，并在规则变化时进行修订、扩展和淘汰。同时构建了基于真实 ServiceNow 环境的基准 EnterpriseWorldShift，包含九张表、25 条隐藏规则和 600 个评估动作，设计了四个版本的世界（固定表与记录，依次修改、新增、删除规则），分别测试模型发现、修订、扩展与淘汰能力。

Result: CDA 能持续构建并迁移世界模型，其对隐藏规则效果的预测准确率相比此前"逐问查询"的方法最高提升 8.98 个 IoU 点，且完全依靠自身模型作答，无需查询正在运行的系统。

Conclusion: 本文验证了持续企业世界模型发现是可行且有效的：通过交互学习构建的世界模型不仅能随业务规则变化持续更新，还能在不查询实时系统的前提下更准确地预测规则效果，为构建适应动态企业环境的智能体提供了新方法。

Abstract: In an enterprise system, updating one field can set another, create a record, or start an approval. These effects are produced by business rules that are not built into the platform but written by each organization and revised over time. An agent working in such a system cannot predict the result of its own actions without knowing these rules. We study continual enterprise world model discovery, where an agent starts without knowledge of these business rules and discovers them by interacting with records and observing the outcomes. From those observations it builds a world model, which it revises as the rules change. To evaluate this, we introduce EnterpriseWorldShift, built on a live ServiceNow environment with nine tables, 25 hidden rules and 600 evaluation actions. It presents four versions of the same enterprise world, with the tables and records held fixed while a rule is modified, then added, then removed, so that discovery, revision, extension and retirement are each tested in turn. Our Continual Discovery Agent (CDA) builds such a model and carries it from one world to the next. It predicts the effects of the hidden rules more accurately than looking them up for each question, the approach taken by prior work, by up to 8.98 IoU points, and it answers from its own model without querying the running system.

</details>


### [200] [SIMLIFE: Pattern Understanding for Long-Horizon Human-Agent Partnership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19610)
*Run Peng,Zinnia Nie,Jing Ding,Yinpei Dai,Yichi Zhang,Zengqing Wu,Yao Fu,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimLife 平台与 SimLife-BP 基准，用于评估智能体对长时程家庭生活行为模式的理解能力。实验发现当前模型仅能做表面预测，缺乏规则推理、过度依赖频率启发式、难以适应模式变化，表明长上下文模式理解仍是具身智能体的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类需要智能体不仅能推断当下的需求，还要理解日常惯例如何形成、为何重复以及何时改变。现有基准多聚焦短期、显式任务，缺乏对长时间跨度（数周至数月）内隐性行为规则理解的评估，因此作者希望构建一个可扩展平台和基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构建了 SimLife——一个可扩展的长期家庭生活模拟平台，提供丰富的视觉观测、真实的动作日志和带音频的合成对话。在其上提出 SimLife-BP 基准，包含 106 个片段（平均 15.49 小时、38.57 游戏内天数）和 1,439 组问答对，从直接推理、反事实推理、含噪推理和逆向推理四个维度，在不同规则提示强度下考察模型对潜在行为规则的推断能力。

Result: 对前沿模型和架构的评测发现：现有模型往往只能实现表面层面的预测而缺乏对规则的全面理解；倾向依赖基于频率的启发式，而非基于证据的 if-then 条件推理；当行为模式发生变化时难以适应调整。

Conclusion: 长上下文模式理解仍是未来具身智能体的主要瓶颈。SimLife 为研究日常人机交互中的记忆、个性化、适应性和长时程规划开辟了更广阔的空间。

Abstract: Understanding humans over long horizons requires agents to infer not only what people need in the moment, but also how routines form, why they repeat, and when they change. We introduce SimLife, a scalable platform for simulating long-term household life with rich visual observations, ground-truth action logs, and synthetic dialogues with audio. Built on SimLife, SimLife-BP evaluates long-context pattern understanding: the ability to infer latent behavioral rules from weeks or months of everyday observations. The benchmark contains 106 episodes averaging 15.49 hours and 38.57 in-game days, and 1,439 question-answer pairs. Each task probes direct, counterfactual, noisy, and inverse reasoning under different levels of rule hints. Evaluating frontier models and architectures, we find that current models often achieve surface-level prediction without comprehensive rule understanding, rely on frequency-based heuristics rather than if-then reasoning over evidence, and struggle to adapt when behavioral patterns change. These findings suggest that long-context pattern understanding remains a major bottleneck for future embodied agents, while SimLife opens a broader space for studying memory, personalization, adaptation, and long-horizon planning in everyday human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [201] [From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19630)
*Diba Afroze,Xingli Zhang,Yazhou Tu,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含202个场景、七类决策标签（执行/拒绝/澄清/要求确认/转交手动/紧急响应/不调用工具）的车辆语音助手授权决策基准，并评估多个LLM的决策对齐与安全性。API模型对齐率达83.2%–89.1%，但即便最佳模型仍产生2–3次误执行，结构化授权策略无法作为独立安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被集成到车辆语音助手中，但将自然语言请求映射到车辆功能会带来安全关键的授权问题。系统在执行指令前必须决定：执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、转交手动控制、触发紧急响应或不调用工具。以往评估未在说话人角色、认证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性等维度上孤立考察这一“执行前决策”，因此缺乏针对性基准。

Method: 提出一个包含202个场景的基准，并配以七类标签体系的“参考决策(Reference Decisions)”。采用“决策对齐(Decision Alignment)”及面向安全的具体错误指标，对两个本地开源权重模型和三个API类LLM进行评测；另设计对照消融实验，对比结构化授权策略与仅模式(schema-only)及通用安全(generic-safety)基线在Llama 3.2 3B上的表现。

Result: 决策对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%。三个API模型得分在83.2%–89.1%之间，两两间无统计学显著差异。即便最优模型，在161个非执行场景中仍产生2到3次误执行(False Executes)，且在确认与手动控制决策上存在持续错误。Llama 3.2 3B在结构化授权策略下对齐率达40.1%，而模式仅与通用安全基线仅为28.2%–29.2%，但结构化策略同样未能消除误执行。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM决策不足以作为独立的安全机制；实际部署必须额外设置独立的执行(执法)层，在调用任何车辆功能前校验工具权限与车辆状态约束。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes.
